"""New profile validation; legacy schema 3/4 contracts keep their own validation."""
import copy
import math
from .. import BACKEND_COMMIT
from .resources import disk_policy

DEFAULT = {
    'schema_version':5,
    'game':{'edition':'bedrock','target_version':'26.52','backend_model':'MC_26_50',
            'backend_commit':BACKEND_COMMIT,'bds_version':'1.26.52.3'},
    'deployment':{'overworld_side_blocks':1536,'nether_side_blocks':896},
    'discovery':{'overworld_bounds':[-2000,-2000,2000,2000],
                 'nether_bounds':[-2000,-2000,2000,2000]},
    'search':{'mode':'prediction','sequence':'permutation','run_key':'0','workers':30,'max_workers':36,
              'numeric_threads_per_worker':1,'native_threads_per_worker':1,'batch_size':32,'pilot_limit':1000,'inventory_mode':'eligibility',
              'memory_target_gib':25,'memory_ceiling_gib':30,'docker_reserve_gib':8,
              'run_disk_limit_bytes':128 << 30,'free_disk_reserve_bytes':16 << 30,
              'projected_bytes_per_seed':65536,
              'structure_order':['ancient_city','village','trial_chamber','mineshaft']},
    'structures':{'required':['stronghold','village','trial_chamber','ancient_city','mineshaft'],
                  'nether_required':['fortress','bastion'],
                  'optional_bonuses':{'monument':5,'outpost':5,'shipwreck':2,'ruined_portal':2,
                                      'buried_treasure':1,'monster_room':1}},
    'biomes':{'spacing':64,'sample_y':63,'lattice':'world','gate_batch_size':16,'refinement_budget':128,'wooded_minimum_families':2,
              'family_bonuses':{'desert':3,'swamp':3,'snowy_ice':2,'badlands':2,'mushroom':3,
                                'plains_meadow':1,'mountains':2,'ocean':2,'rivers_shores':1},
              'variant_bonuses':{'pale_garden':3,'mangrove_swamp':3,'bamboo_jungle':2,
                                 'dappled_forest':2,'warm_ocean':3}},
    'scoring':{'geometry_weight':.8,'biome_fit_weight':.1,'baseline_weight':.1,'baseline_quality_cap':60},
    'terrain':{'variants':['v1','v2'],'native_threads':1,'scheduler':'rotation','resolutions':[128,64,32,16,8,4],
               'center_spacing':64,'refine_center_spacing':16,'rays':32,
               'maximum_refinements_per_anchor':8},
    'bds':{'enabled':True,'version':'1.26.52.3',
           'docker_cli':'/Applications/Docker.app/Contents/Resources/bin/docker',
           'image':'bedrock-verification-poc:1.26.52.3',
           'image_id':'sha256:cf479afd0ab8a8bb095f7aa71fd42064cf5ba7aff6c8a8f4f5503aac826511d8',
           'cpus':4,'memory_gib':6,'verification_budget':20,'persist_snapshot':False,
           'compare_reference':True,'maximum_region_side':256},
    'caves':{'automatic':False,'y_levels':[0,-16,-32,-48],'spacing':64,'maximum_samples':10000},
}


def default_config():
    cfg=copy.deepcopy(DEFAULT)
    from .terrain import SEARCH_V1, SEARCH_V2
    cfg['terrain']['v1']=dict(SEARCH_V1)
    cfg['terrain']['v2']=dict(SEARCH_V2)
    return cfg


def _integer(value, lo, hi, name):
    if isinstance(value,bool) or not isinstance(value,int) or not lo<=value<=hi:
        raise ValueError(f'{name} must be an integer in {lo}–{hi}')


def memory_budget_gib(search, *, docker_reserve_default=8):
    """Validate the combined scanner/Docker budget before checkpoint mutation."""
    values=[search.get('memory_target_gib',25),search.get('memory_ceiling_gib',30),
            search.get('docker_reserve_gib',docker_reserve_default)]
    for name,value in zip(('memory_target_gib','memory_ceiling_gib','docker_reserve_gib'),values):
        if isinstance(value,bool) or not isinstance(value,(int,float)) or (isinstance(value,float) and not math.isfinite(value)):
            raise ValueError(f'{name} must be a finite number')
    target,ceiling,reserve=values
    if not 0<target<=ceiling<=30:
        raise ValueError('Memory target must fit the agreed 30 GiB combined ceiling')
    if not 0<=reserve<ceiling:
        raise ValueError('Docker reserve must be nonnegative and below the combined memory ceiling')
    return target,ceiling,reserve


def validate_config(cfg):
    if cfg.get('schema_version')!=5:
        raise ValueError('Realm profile requires schema_version 5')
    for name in DEFAULT:
        if name not in cfg:raise ValueError(f'Missing Realm configuration section {name}')
    for key,value in DEFAULT['game'].items():
        if cfg['game'].get(key)!=value:raise ValueError(f'Unsupported or unpinned game setting {key}')
    if cfg['deployment'] != DEFAULT['deployment']:
        raise ValueError('This Realm contract uses 1536 Overworld and 896 Nether blocks')
    if cfg['discovery'] != DEFAULT['discovery']:
        raise ValueError('Discovery must use exactly half-open [-2000,2000) in each dimension')
    s=cfg['search']
    if s.get('mode')!='prediction' or s.get('sequence') not in ('permutation','sequential','manifest'):
        raise ValueError('Realm scan mode must explicitly label predictions and supported seed sequence')
    if s.get('sequence')=='manifest':
        from ..seeds import parse_seed
        values=s.get('manifest_seeds')
        if not isinstance(values,list) or not 1<=len(values)<=10000 or any(not isinstance(v,str) or str(parse_seed(v))!=v for v in values):
            raise ValueError('Manifest requires 1–10000 canonical signed decimal seeds')
        if len(set(values))!=len(values):raise ValueError('Manifest seeds must be distinct')
    if s.get('inventory_mode','complete') not in ('complete','eligibility'):raise ValueError('Invalid inventory mode')
    if cfg['biomes'].get('lattice','border') not in ('border','world'):raise ValueError('Invalid biome lattice')
    _integer(cfg['biomes'].get('gate_batch_size',16),1,256,'biome gate batch size')
    _integer(cfg['biomes'].get('refinement_budget',0),0,4096,'biome refinement budget')
    key=s.get('run_key')
    if not isinstance(key,str) or not key.isdigit() or not 0<=int(key)<2**64:
        raise ValueError('run_key must be a decimal uint64 string')
    if s['sequence']=='sequential' and int(key)!=0:raise ValueError('Sequential scans require run_key 0')
    _integer(s.get('max_workers'),1,36,'max_workers');_integer(s.get('workers'),1,s['max_workers'],'workers')
    _integer(s.get('batch_size'),1,4096,'batch_size');_integer(s.get('pilot_limit'),1,1000,'pilot_limit')
    if s.get('numeric_threads_per_worker')!=1:raise ValueError('Use exactly one numeric thread per worker')
    _integer(s.get('native_threads_per_worker',1),1,36,'native_threads_per_worker')
    if s['workers']*s.get('native_threads_per_worker',1)>36:
        raise ValueError('Process/native thread product must fit 36 logical CPUs')
    if 'memory_target_gib' not in s or 'memory_ceiling_gib' not in s:
        raise ValueError('Memory target and ceiling must be configured')
    memory_budget_gib(s)
    disk_policy(s)
    if cfg.get('terrain',{}).get('scheduler','rotation') not in ('rotation','priority'):
        raise ValueError('Terrain scheduler must be rotation or priority')
    _integer(cfg.get('terrain',{}).get('native_threads',1),1,36,'terrain.native_threads')
    order=s.get('structure_order',[])
    if len(order)!=4 or set(order)!=set(DEFAULT['search']['structure_order']):
        raise ValueError('All four mandatory non-anchor structures must be checked')
    if cfg['structures'].get('required')!=DEFAULT['structures']['required']:
        raise ValueError('Required structure eligibility cannot be relaxed')
    if cfg['structures'].get('nether_required',DEFAULT['structures']['nether_required'])!=['fortress','bastion']:
        raise ValueError('Both Fortress and Bastion are mandatory in the final Nether square')
    for section,keys in (('structures',('optional_bonuses',)),('biomes',('family_bonuses','variant_bonuses'))):
        for key in keys:
            vals=cfg[section].get(key,{})
            if set(vals)!=set(DEFAULT[section][key]) or any(isinstance(v,bool) or not isinstance(v,(int,float)) or not math.isfinite(v) or v<0 for v in vals.values()):
                raise ValueError(f'Invalid configurable {key}')
    if cfg['biomes'].get('wooded_minimum_families')!=2:raise ValueError('At least two wooded families remain mandatory')
    if cfg['biomes'].get('spacing') not in (4,8,16,32,64,128):raise ValueError('Invalid biome spacing')
    if cfg['biomes'].get('sample_y')!=63:raise ValueError('Fixed-Y predictor must retain declared Y=63 provenance')
    t=cfg['terrain']
    if t.get('variants')!=['v1','v2'] or t.get('rays')!=32:raise ValueError('Only independent V1/V2 with 32 rays are supported')
    if t.get('resolutions')!=[128,64,32,16,8,4]:raise ValueError('Keep the declared adaptive terrain resolutions')
    _integer(t.get('maximum_refinements_per_anchor'),0,10000,'maximum_refinements_per_anchor')
    for key in ('center_spacing','refine_center_spacing'):_integer(t.get(key),1,256,key)
    from .terrain import V1,V2
    for name,defaults in (('v1',V1),('v2',V2)):
        if name in t:
            values=t[name]
            required=set(defaults)-({'ridge_min_arc_rays','ridge_min_arc_aspect','enclosure_policy'} if name=='v2' else set())
            if not isinstance(values,dict) or not required.issubset(values) or set(values)-set(defaults) or any(isinstance(v,bool) or not isinstance(v,(int,float)) or not math.isfinite(v) or v<=0 for k,v in values.items() if k!='enclosure_policy'):
                raise ValueError(f'Invalid configurable {name} geometry thresholds')
            if name=='v2':
                _integer(values.get('ridge_min_arc_rays',defaults['ridge_min_arc_rays']),1,t['rays'],'ridge_min_arc_rays')
                if values.get('enclosure_policy','continuous_arcs') not in ('continuous_arcs','surrounding_mountains'):
                    raise ValueError('Invalid configurable v2 enclosure_policy')
    weights=[cfg['scoring'].get(n) for n in ('geometry_weight','biome_fit_weight','baseline_weight')]
    if any(isinstance(v,bool) or not isinstance(v,(int,float)) or not math.isfinite(v) or v<0 for v in weights) or abs(sum(weights)-1)>1e-9:
        raise ValueError('Variant weights must be finite, nonnegative and sum to one')
    if cfg['scoring']['baseline_quality_cap']<=0:raise ValueError('Baseline quality cap must be positive')
    if cfg['caves'].get('automatic') is not False:raise ValueError('Cave inventory requires explicit manual invocation')
    if cfg['caves'].get('y_levels')!=[0,-16,-32,-48]:raise ValueError('Keep the agreed initial cave Y levels')
    _integer(cfg['caves'].get('maximum_samples'),1,1000000,'maximum_samples')
    if cfg['caves'].get('spacing') not in (4,8,16,32,64,128):raise ValueError('Invalid manual cave spacing')
    b=cfg['bds']
    for key in ('version','image_id','image'):
        if b.get(key)!=DEFAULT['bds'][key]:raise ValueError(f'BDS {key} must match the verified pin')
    from .verification import validate_bds_pins
    validate_bds_pins(cfg)
    _integer(b.get('cpus'),4,4,'BDS CPUs');_integer(b.get('verification_budget'),0,10000,'verification_budget')
    if b.get('memory_gib')!=6:raise ValueError('Tested BDS allocation requires exactly 6 GiB memory')
    return cfg


========================================================================
FILE: src/seed_search/realm/baseline.py
========================================================================
"""Cheap universal baseline; predictions qualify provisionally, negatives remain pending."""
import os
import platform
import resource
import time
import numpy as np
from ..backend import Backend, STRUCTURES
from ..seeds import parse_seed
from .bounds import square, contains, native_bounds, nether_square, json_bounds
from .biomes import postprocess_ids
from .capabilities import report
from .sampling import BiomeSampler


def structure_observation(backend, name, bounds, combined=True, *, minimal=False, pair=None):
    if name not in STRUCTURES:
        return {'type':name,'bounds':json_bounds(bounds),'raw':[], 'predicted_viable':[],
                'coordinates':[],'data_status':'UNSUPPORTED','verification':'UNVERIFIED',
                'confirmed_absent':False,'complete':False}
    nb=native_bounds(bounds)
    partial=False
    if pair is not None:raw,viable=pair
    elif minimal and hasattr(backend,'structure_first') and name!='stronghold':
        point=backend.structure_first(name,nb)
        raw=viable=[point] if point is not None else []
        partial=bool(point)
    elif minimal and name=='mineshaft' and hasattr(backend,'mineshaft_first'):
        point=backend.mineshaft_first(nb)
        raw=viable=[point] if point is not None else []
        partial=bool(point)
    elif combined:raw,viable=backend.structure_pair(name,nb)
    else:raw,viable=backend.structures(name,nb,False),backend.structures(name,nb,True)
    raw=sorted({tuple(p) for p in raw if contains(p,bounds)})
    viable=sorted({tuple(p) for p in viable if contains(p,bounds)})
    return {'type':name,'bounds':json_bounds(bounds),'raw':[list(p) for p in raw],
            'predicted_viable':[list(p) for p in viable],'coordinates':[list(p) for p in viable],
            'data_status':'PREDICTED' if viable else 'PARTIAL',
            'verification':'UNVERIFIED','confirmed_absent':False,
            'complete':False,'inventory_status':'DEFERRED' if partial else 'COMPLETE',
            'detection_method':'first_viable_start' if minimal else 'complete_predictor_starts',
            'evidence_scope':'positive witness; remaining starts unqueried' if partial else 'bounded predictor domain',
            'negative_meaning':'not found by unvalidated predictor; not confirmed absent'}


def baseline_score_uncertainty(biomes, optional, cfg, pending):
    """Bonus bounds do not establish required-structure or wooded eligibility."""
    optional_max = sum(cfg['structures']['optional_bonuses'].values())
    optional_observed = sum(v.get('bonus', 0) for v in optional.values())
    biome_max = 15 + sum(cfg['biomes']['family_bonuses'].values()) + sum(cfg['biomes']['variant_bonuses'].values())
    biome_prediction = biomes.get('uncertainty', {}).get('score_interval', [biomes['score'], biome_max]) if biomes else [0, biome_max]
    biome_actual = biomes.get('uncertainty', {}).get('actual_generation_score_interval', [0, biome_max]) if biomes else [0, biome_max]
    return {'domain': 'prediction_model_only',
            'optional_prediction_interval': [optional_observed, optional_max],
            'optional_actual_generation_interval': [0, optional_max],
            'biome_prediction_interval': biome_prediction,
            'biome_actual_generation_interval': biome_actual,
            'prediction_score_interval': [biome_prediction[0] + optional_observed, biome_prediction[1] + optional_max],
            'actual_generation_score_interval': [biome_actual[0], biome_actual[1] + optional_max],
            'unresolved_required_eligibility': ['initial_fortress', *cfg['structures']['required'], 'final_fortress','final_bastion','wooded_family_coverage'],
            'pending_prediction_requirements': list(pending),
            'eligibility_verified': False,
            'interpretation': 'Observed predictor bonuses do not lower-bound actual generation; bonus intervals do not resolve required eligibility or discovery recall.'}


def evaluate_seed(index, seed, cfg, *, backend_factory=Backend, combined=True, short_circuit=True):
    seed=parse_seed(seed);start=time.perf_counter();cpu_start=time.process_time();times={};counts={};anchors=[]
    minimal=cfg['search'].get('inventory_mode','complete')=='eligibility'
    def timed(name, fn):
        t=time.perf_counter()
        try:return fn()
        finally:times[name]=times.get(name,0)+time.perf_counter()-t
    with backend_factory(seed) as backend:
        sampler=BiomeSampler(backend)
        ob=cfg['discovery']['overworld_bounds'];nb=cfg['discovery']['nether_bounds']
        strongholds=timed('stronghold',lambda:structure_observation(backend,'stronghold',ob,combined))
        fortress=timed('initial_fortress',lambda:structure_observation(backend,'fortress',nb,combined,minimal=minimal))
        counts['stronghold_anchors']=len(strongholds['predicted_viable'])
        counts['initial_fortress_predicted']=int(bool(fortress['predicted_viable']))
        for anchor in strongholds['predicted_viable']:
            bounds=square(anchor,cfg['deployment']['overworld_side_blocks'])
            nether_bounds=nether_square(anchor,cfg['deployment']['nether_side_blocks'])
            required={'stronghold':{**strongholds,'bounds':json_bounds(bounds),
                                   'coordinates':[anchor],'predicted_viable':[anchor],'raw':[anchor]}}
            pending=[]
            if not fortress['predicted_viable']:pending.append('initial_fortress')
            nether_required={}
            pairs=timed('final_nether_pair',lambda:backend.nether_structure_pairs(native_bounds(nether_bounds))) if hasattr(backend,'nether_structure_pairs') else None
            for name in ('fortress','bastion'):
                nether_required[name]=timed('final_'+name,lambda n=name:structure_observation(backend,n,nether_bounds,combined,pair=pairs[n] if pairs else None))
                if not nether_required[name]['predicted_viable']:
                    pending.append('final_'+name)
                    counts['pending_final_'+name]=counts.get('pending_final_'+name,0)+1
            for name in cfg['search']['structure_order']:
                if pending and short_circuit:
                    required[name]={'type':name,'bounds':json_bounds(bounds),'raw':[],
                                    'coordinates':[],'predicted_viable':[], 'data_status':'PARTIAL',
                                    'verification':'DEFERRED','confirmed_absent':False,'complete':False}
                    continue
                required[name]=timed(name,lambda n=name:structure_observation(backend,n,bounds,combined,minimal=minimal))
                if not required[name]['predicted_viable']:
                    pending.append(name);counts['pending_'+name]=counts.get('pending_'+name,0)+1
            optional={};biomes=None
            if not pending or not short_circuit:
                before=sampler.queried
                biomes=timed('wooded_biomes',lambda:sampler.gate(bounds,cfg['biomes'],complete=not minimal))
                counts['biome_samples']=counts.get('biome_samples',0)+sampler.queried-before
                if biomes['qualification']=='PENDING':pending.append('wooded_family_coverage')
            if not pending and not minimal:
                for name,bonus in cfg['structures']['optional_bonuses'].items():
                    optional[name]=timed('optional_structures',lambda n=name:structure_observation(backend,n,bounds,combined))
                    optional[name]['bonus']=bonus if optional[name]['predicted_viable'] else 0
                    optional[name]['bonus_status']='PREDICTED' if optional[name]['predicted_viable'] else 'unknown; zero observed lower bound'
            status='PENDING' if pending else 'PREDICTED'
            bonus=(biomes['score'] if biomes else 0)+sum(v['bonus'] for v in optional.values())
            inventory_deferred=minimal
            tasks=['structures','biomes'] if minimal else []
            anchors.append({'id':f'{seed}:{anchor[0]}:{anchor[1]}','index':index,'seed':str(seed),'stronghold':anchor,
                            'game_version':cfg['game']['target_version'],'bds_version':cfg['game']['bds_version'],
                            'backend_model':cfg['game']['backend_model'],'backend_commit':cfg['game']['backend_commit'],
                            'bounds':{'overworld':json_bounds(bounds),'initial_overworld':list(ob),'initial_nether':list(nb),
                                      'final_nether':json_bounds(nether_bounds)},
                            'required_structures':required,'optional_structures':optional,'biomes':biomes,
                            'initial_fortress':fortress,'required_nether_structures':nether_required,
                            'final_nether_prediction':{'required':['fortress','bastion'],
                                'status':'PREDICTED' if all(v['predicted_viable'] for v in nether_required.values()) else 'PENDING',
                                'bounds':json_bounds(nether_bounds),'actual_generation_verified':False},
                            'baseline_score':None if inventory_deferred else bonus,'baseline_score_observed':bonus,
                            'baseline_score_complete':not inventory_deferred,
                            'baseline_score_status':'PARTIAL; inventory deferred' if inventory_deferred else 'PREDICTED; observed predictor score',
                            'inventory':{'version':'candidate-inventory-v1','status':'DEFERRED' if inventory_deferred else 'COMPLETE' if not pending else 'UNRESOLVED',
                                         'completion_scope':'predictor family presence and canonical biome sample; complete start counts require explicit audit; actual generation unverified',
                                         'tasks':tasks,'sampling_policy':{k:cfg['biomes'][k] for k in ('spacing','sample_y','lattice','gate_batch_size','refinement_budget') if k in cfg['biomes']}},
                            'baseline_score_uncertainty':baseline_score_uncertainty(biomes,optional,cfg,pending),
                            'qualification':status,'data_status':'PREDICTED','processing_status':'eligibility_complete_inventory_deferred' if inventory_deferred else 'baseline_complete' if not pending else 'baseline_pending',
                            'pending_reasons':pending,'final_nether_status':'UNVERIFIED',
                            'terrain_status':{'v1':'not_evaluated','v2':'not_evaluated'}})
        counts['baseline_predicted']=sum(a['qualification']=='PREDICTED' for a in anchors)
        counts['baseline_pending']=sum(a['qualification']=='PENDING' for a in anchors)
        counts['confirmed_rejections']=0
        counts['unresolved_discovery']=int(not strongholds['predicted_viable'])
        counts['unresolved_stronghold_coverage']=1
        counts['unresolved_fortress_coverage']=1
        work=getattr(backend,'native_work',{})
    rss=resource.getrusage(resource.RUSAGE_SELF).ru_maxrss*(1 if platform.system()=='Darwin' else 1024)
    return {'index':index,'seed':str(seed),'anchors':anchors,'counts':counts,'stage_seconds':times,
            'worker_cpu_seconds':time.process_time()-cpu_start,'biome_cache_hits':sampler.hits,
            'total_seconds':time.perf_counter()-start,'worker_pid':os.getpid(),'worker_peak_rss_bytes':rss,
            'native_work':work,'discovery_status':'PREDICTED' if anchors else 'PENDING',
            'discovery':{'stronghold':strongholds,'fortress':fortress,
                         'seed':str(seed),'settings':dict(cfg['discovery']),
                         'source':{'kind':'prediction','provider':'native',
                                   'target_version':cfg['game']['target_version'],
                                   'version':cfg['game']['backend_model'],
                                   'backend_commit':cfg['game']['backend_commit']},
                         'coverage':{'status':'UNRESOLVED','stronghold_recall':'UNVALIDATED',
                                     'fortress_recall':'UNVALIDATED','authoritative_absence':False,
                                     'complete':False}},
            'effective_seed_bits':{'viability':64,'raw_MT_placement':32}}


========================================================================
FILE: src/seed_search/realm/inventory.py
========================================================================
"""Durable complete scoring inventories and positive witnesses for extra families."""
import fcntl
import os
import time

from ..backend import Backend, STRUCTURES, configure_native_threads, biome_catalog
from .baseline import structure_observation
from .bounds import native_bounds
from .sampling import BiomeSampler, grid_points, dispersed_indices
from .resources import disk_policy, disk_status

NETHER_TYPES = {'fortress', 'bastion', 'ruined_portal_nether'}
END_TYPES = {'end_city'}


def detect_structures(candidate, cfg, backend, *, exhaustive=False):
    bounds = candidate['bounds']['overworld']
    final_nether = candidate['bounds']['final_nether']
    observations = {}
    timings = {}
    required = dict(candidate['required_structures'])
    optional = {}
    pairs = backend.nether_structure_pairs(native_bounds(final_nether)) if hasattr(backend, 'nether_structure_pairs') else None
    for name in STRUCTURES:
        if name == 'stronghold':
            started=time.perf_counter()
            observations[name] = structure_observation(backend,name,bounds) if exhaustive else required[name]
            observations[name] = {**observations[name], 'dimension':'overworld',
                                  'scope':'final border; predictor recall remains incomplete' if exhaustive else 'saved discovery anchor'}
            timings[name]=time.perf_counter()-started
            continue
        area = (cfg.get('discovery', {}).get('overworld_bounds', [-2000, -2000, 2000, 2000]) if name in END_TYPES else
                final_nether if name in NETHER_TYPES else bounds)
        # Presence is sufficient for requirements and once-per-family bonuses.
        # Complete start counts remain an explicit, separately costed audit.
        minimal = not exhaustive and name not in ('fortress', 'bastion')
        started = time.perf_counter()
        observation = structure_observation(backend, name, area, minimal=minimal,
                                            pair=pairs[name] if pairs and name in pairs else None)
        timings[name] = time.perf_counter()-started
        observation['dimension'] = 'end' if name in END_TYPES else 'nether' if name in NETHER_TYPES else 'overworld'
        observation['scope'] = 'supplemental discovery; not Realm eligibility' if name in END_TYPES else 'final border'
        observations[name] = observation
        if name in required:
            required[name] = observation
    for name, bonus in cfg['structures']['optional_bonuses'].items():
        observation = observations.get(name) or structure_observation(backend, name, bounds)
        optional[name] = {**observation, 'bonus': bonus if observation['predicted_viable'] else 0,
                          'bonus_status': 'PREDICTED' if observation['predicted_viable'] else 'unknown; zero observed lower bound'}
        if name not in observations:
            observations[name] = observation
    return {'required_structures': required, 'optional_structures': optional,
            'required_nether_structures': {name: observations[name] for name in ('fortress', 'bastion')},
            'structure_inventory': observations, 'structure_inventory_seconds': timings}


def complete_start_audit(store, cfg, candidate_id):
    """Explicit complete native inventory, independent of the witness queue."""
    from .capabilities import identity
    if store.identity.get('backend') and store.identity['backend'] != identity():
        raise ValueError('Complete-start audit backend identity changed')
    candidate=store.get_candidate(candidate_id)
    if candidate is None:raise ValueError('Unknown candidate')
    begin=time.perf_counter()
    with Backend(candidate['seed']) as backend:
        patch=detect_structures(candidate,cfg,backend,exhaustive=True)
    # A full inventory is an additional revision; it cannot replace the frozen
    # minimal evidence or falsely imply actual-generation completeness.
    observation={'kind':'complete_predictor_start_inventory','candidate_id':candidate_id,
                 'backend':identity(),'structures':patch['structure_inventory'],
                 'seconds':time.perf_counter()-begin,'actual_generation_verified':False}
    store.upsert_observation(candidate_id,observation)
    return observation


def execute_inventory(candidate, cfg, kind, backend, *, exhaustive=False):
    if kind == 'structures':
        return detect_structures(candidate, cfg, backend, exhaustive=exhaustive)
    if kind != 'biomes':
        raise ValueError('Unknown inventory kind')
    sampler = BiomeSampler(backend)
    policy = candidate['inventory'].get('sampling_policy', cfg['biomes'])
    # Use the current score weights, retaining the frozen generation/sampling policy.
    policy = {**cfg['biomes'], **policy, 'family_bonuses': cfg['biomes']['family_bonuses'], 'variant_bonuses': cfg['biomes']['variant_bonuses']}
    biomes = sampler.gate(candidate['bounds']['overworld'], policy, complete=True,
                         prior=(candidate.get('biomes') or {}).get('sampling'))
    additional = {}
    for dimension, label, y, spacing, bounds in (
            (-1, 'nether', 33, 64, candidate['bounds']['final_nether']),
            (1, 'end', 63, 128, cfg['discovery']['overworld_bounds'])):
        points = grid_points(bounds, spacing, y, 'world')
        expected=len(points)
        active={item['id'] for item in biome_catalog() if item['generates'] and item['dimension']==dimension}
        if exhaustive:
            values=sampler.sample(points,dimension)
        else:
            sampled=[];ids=[];seen=set()
            order=dispersed_indices(len(points))
            for offset in range(0,len(order),16):
                batch=points[order[offset:offset+16]]
                values=sampler.sample(batch,dimension)
                sampled.extend(batch);ids.extend(map(int,values));seen.update(map(int,values))
                if active<=seen:break
            import numpy as np
            points=np.array(sampled,dtype=np.int32).reshape(-1,3)
            values=np.array(ids,dtype=np.int32)
        from .biomes import postprocess_ids
        item = postprocess_ids(values, points, policy)
        item.update(bounds=list(bounds), sample_y=y, spacing=spacing,
                    sample_count=len(points), expected_grid_points=expected, dimension=label,
                    complete=False, missing_model_ids=sorted(active-set(map(int,values))),
                    detection_method='full_defined_grid' if exhaustive else 'dispersed_positive_class_witnesses',
                    coverage='bounded sample; unseen biomes unresolved, never absent')
        additional[label] = item
    return {'biomes': biomes, 'supplemental_biome_inventories': additional}


def run_inventory(store, cfg, *, limit=None, candidate_id=None, include_pending=False, exhaustive=False, progress=None):
    """One scheduler owns recovery; leases and candidate patches commit together."""
    from .capabilities import identity
    if store.identity.get('backend') and store.identity['backend'] != identity():
        raise ValueError('Inventory backend identity changed; use a fresh compatible run')
    configure_native_threads(cfg['search'].get('native_threads_per_worker', 1))
    if limit is not None and (isinstance(limit, bool) or not isinstance(limit, int) or limit < 0):
        raise ValueError('Inventory limit must be nonnegative')
    # Exhaustive supplemental enumeration is an explicit audit, not a resume of
    # a presence-mode task. Use a fresh computation identity for such requests.
    if exhaustive:
        raise ValueError('Use detect_structures(..., exhaustive=True) in an explicit audit; durable inventory uses its frozen presence policy')
    if candidate_id is not None and store.get_candidate(candidate_id) is None:
        raise ValueError('Unknown inventory candidate')
    started = time.monotonic(); cpu = time.process_time(); completed = 0; failed = 0
    lock = (store.run/'inventory-scheduler.lock').open('a+')
    try:
        try:
            fcntl.flock(lock.fileno(), fcntl.LOCK_EX | fcntl.LOCK_NB)
        except BlockingIOError:
            raise ValueError('Another inventory scheduler owns this checkpoint') from None
        store.recover_inventory_work()
        while limit is None or completed < limit:
            unit = store.claim_inventory_work(f'inventory-{os.getpid()}', candidate_id, include_pending=include_pending)
            if unit is None:
                break
            try:
                disk_status(store.run, disk_policy(cfg['search']))
                candidate = store.get_candidate(unit['candidate_id'])
                with Backend(candidate['seed']) as backend:
                    patch = execute_inventory(candidate, store.get_state('score_policy') or cfg, unit['kind'], backend)
                store.complete_inventory_work(unit, patch)
                completed += 1
                if progress:
                    progress({'completed_inventory_tasks': completed, 'candidate': candidate['id'], 'kind': unit['kind']})
            except Exception as error:
                store.fail_inventory_work(unit, error)
                failed += 1
                raise
    finally:
        lock.close()
    return {'completed_tasks': completed, 'failed_tasks': failed,
            'seconds': time.monotonic()-started, 'cpu_seconds': time.process_time()-cpu,
            'inventory_work': store.status()['inventory_work']}


========================================================================
FILE: src/seed_search/realm/sampling.py
========================================================================
"""Deterministic biome witnesses; canonical inventory and supplemental evidence differ."""
import math

import numpy as np

from ..backend import biome_name
from .biomes import WOOD, canonical, postprocess_ids, classify
from .bounds import contains


def grid_points(bounds, spacing, y, lattice='world'):
    if lattice not in ('border', 'world'):
        raise ValueError('Unknown biome lattice')
    x0, z0, x1, z1 = bounds
    if lattice == 'world':
        x0 = math.ceil(x0/spacing)*spacing
        z0 = math.ceil(z0/spacing)*spacing
    x, z = np.meshgrid(np.arange(x0, x1, spacing, dtype=np.int32),
                       np.arange(z0, z1, spacing, dtype=np.int32))
    return np.ascontiguousarray(np.column_stack((x.ravel(), np.full(x.size, y), z.ravel())), dtype=np.int32)


def dispersed_indices(n):
    if not n:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.int32)
    stride = max(1, int(n*.618033988749895))
    while math.gcd(stride, n) != 1:
        stride += 1
    return (np.arange(n, dtype=np.int64)*stride % n).astype(np.int32)


def wooded_ids(values):
    names = {canonical(biome_name(int(i))) for i in values}
    return {family for family, members in WOOD.items() if names.intersection(members)}


class BiomeSampler:
    """Full-seed, context-local cache. No high32 sibling reuse or leaf hints."""
    def __init__(self, backend, maximum=65536):
        self.backend = backend
        self.maximum = maximum
        self.cache = {}
        self.queried = 0
        self.hits = 0

    def sample(self, points, dimension=0):
        missing = list(dict.fromkeys((dimension, *map(int, p)) for p in points
                                    if (dimension, *map(int, p)) not in self.cache))
        fetched = {}
        for offset in range(0, len(missing), 100000):
            batch = missing[offset:offset+100000]
            xyz = np.array([p[1:] for p in batch], dtype=np.int32)
            values = self.backend.biome_points(xyz) if dimension == 0 else self.backend.biome_points(xyz, dimension)
            if len(values) != len(batch):
                raise ValueError('Biome provider returned an incomplete sample batch')
            fetched.update(zip(batch, map(int, values)))
        self.queried += len(missing)
        self.hits += len(points)-len(missing)
        values = np.array([fetched.get((dimension, *map(int, p)),
                           self.cache.get((dimension, *map(int, p)))) for p in points], dtype=np.int32)
        self.cache.update(fetched)
        # Bounded lifetime and memory. Witnesses already emitted into candidate
        # records remain available even when cache entries are evicted.
        while len(self.cache) > self.maximum:
            self.cache.pop(next(iter(self.cache)))
        return values

    def gate(self, bounds, policy, *, complete=False, prior=None):
        step = policy['spacing']; y = policy['sample_y']
        lattice = policy.get('lattice', 'world')
        points = grid_points(bounds, step, y, lattice)
        if prior and any(prior.get(k) != v for k, v in
                         (('version', 'biome-lattice-v2'), ('lattice', lattice), ('spacing', step), ('y', y), ('expected_points', len(points)))):
            raise ValueError('Saved biome sampling identity differs')
        saved = {int(row['index']): int(row['id']) for row in (prior or {}).get('canonical_samples', [])}
        for i, value in saved.items():
            if not 0 <= i < len(points):
                raise ValueError('Saved sample index outside canonical lattice')
            self.cache[(0, *map(int, points[i]))] = value
        supplemental = {(tuple(row['coordinate']), int(row['id']))
                        for row in (prior or {}).get('supplemental_samples', [])
                        if contains((row['coordinate'][0], row['coordinate'][2]), bounds)}
        if lattice == 'world':
            canonical_coords = set(map(tuple, points.tolist()))
            for (dimension, x, py, z), value in self.cache.items():
                if dimension == 0 and py == y and contains((x, z), bounds) and (x, py, z) not in canonical_coords:
                    if wooded_ids([value]):
                        supplemental.add(((x, py, z), value))
        indices = np.arange(len(points), dtype=np.int32) if complete else dispersed_indices(len(points))
        families = wooded_ids([*saved.values(), *(v for p, v in supplemental)])
        for offset in range(0, len(indices), 100000 if complete else policy.get('gate_batch_size', 16)):
            if not complete and len(families) >= 2:
                break
            selected = [int(i) for i in indices[offset:offset+(100000 if complete else policy.get('gate_batch_size', 16))] if int(i) not in saved]
            if not selected:
                continue
            values = self.sample(points[selected])
            saved.update(zip(selected, map(int, values)))
            families.update(wooded_ids(values))
        if not complete and len(families) < 2 and lattice == 'world' and policy.get('refinement_budget', 128):
            finer = grid_points(bounds, max(4, step//2), y, 'world')
            known = set(map(tuple, points.tolist())) | {p for p, v in supplemental}
            finer = np.array([p for p in finer[dispersed_indices(len(finer))] if tuple(p) not in known], dtype=np.int32).reshape(-1, 3)
            budget = min(len(finer), policy.get('refinement_budget', 128))
            for offset in range(0, budget, 16):
                batch = finer[offset:min(offset+16, budget)]
                values = self.sample(batch)
                supplemental.update((tuple(map(int, p)), int(v)) for p, v in zip(batch, values))
                families.update(wooded_ids(values))
                if len(families) >= 2:
                    break
        ordered = sorted(saved)
        result = postprocess_ids(np.array([saved[i] for i in ordered], dtype=np.int32), points[ordered], policy)
        if supplemental:
            # Supplemental positives affect presence, never canonical frequency.
            extras = classify([*result['raw_names'], *(biome_name(v) for p, v in supplemental)], policy)
            detections = result['raw_detections']
            result.update(extras)
            result['raw_detections'] = detections
        result.update(bounds=list(bounds), spacing=step, sample_y=y, sample_count=len(saved),
                      data_status='PREDICTED', source='MC_26_50 fixed-Y biome prediction; not exact surface generation',
                      inventory_status='COMPLETE' if len(saved) == len(points) else 'DEFERRED',
                      sampling={'version': 'biome-lattice-v2', 'lattice': lattice, 'spacing': step, 'y': y,
                                'expected_points': len(points), 'canonical_samples': [{'index': i, 'id': saved[i]} for i in ordered],
                                'supplemental_samples': [{'coordinate': list(p), 'id': v} for p, v in sorted(supplemental)],
                                'frequency_domain': 'canonical lattice only', 'order': 'dispersed-coprime-v1'})
        result['witnesses'] = [{'coordinate': points[i].tolist(), 'id': saved[i], 'biome': biome_name(saved[i])}
                               for i in ordered if wooded_ids([saved[i]])]
        result['witnesses'] += [{'coordinate': list(p), 'id': v, 'biome': biome_name(v), 'supplemental': True}
                                for p, v in sorted(supplemental) if wooded_ids([v])]
        return result


========================================================================
FILE: src/seed_search/realm/biomes.py

"""Five custom wooded families and once-per-family/variant surface rewards."""
from ..backend import biome_name
import numpy as np

ALIASES={
    # Bedrock string identifiers from Mojang's vanilla biome definitions.
    # Numeric IDs are never inferred from this name/family projection.
    'forest_hills':'forest','birch_forest_mutated':'old_growth_birch_forest',
    'birch_forest_hills_mutated':'old_growth_birch_forest',
    'roofed_forest':'dark_forest','roofed_forest_mutated':'dark_forest',
    'cold_taiga':'snowy_taiga','cold_taiga_hills':'snowy_taiga','cold_taiga_mutated':'snowy_taiga',
    'mega_taiga':'old_growth_pine_taiga','mega_taiga_hills':'old_growth_pine_taiga',
    'mega_spruce_taiga':'old_growth_spruce_taiga','mega_spruce_taiga_mutated':'old_growth_spruce_taiga',
    'mega_taiga_mutated':'taiga','redwood_taiga_mutated':'taiga','redwood_taiga_hills_mutated':'taiga','taiga_mutated':'taiga',
    'jungle_mutated':'jungle','jungle_edge_mutated':'sparse_jungle',
    'extreme_hills_plus_trees':'windswept_forest','extreme_hills_plus_trees_mutated':'windswept_forest',
    'extreme_hills':'windswept_hills','extreme_hills_mutated':'windswept_gravelly_hills',
    'extreme_hills_edge':'mountain_edge','swampland':'swamp','swampland_mutated':'swamp',
    'ice_plains':'snowy_plains','ice_plains_spikes':'ice_spikes','ice_mountains':'snowy_mountains',
    'stone_beach':'stony_shore','cold_beach':'snowy_beach','mushroom_island':'mushroom_fields',
    'mushroom_island_shore':'mushroom_field_shore','legacy_frozen_ocean':'frozen_ocean',
    'savanna_mutated':'windswept_savanna','savanna_plateau_mutated':'windswept_savanna',
    'mesa':'badlands','mesa_bryce':'eroded_badlands','mesa_mutated':'badlands',
    'mesa_plateau':'badlands_plateau','mesa_plateau_mutated':'modified_badlands_plateau',
    'mesa_plateau_stone':'wooded_badlands','mesa_plateau_stone_mutated':'wooded_badlands',
    'desert_mutated':'desert_lakes',
    'wooded_hills':'forest','birch_forest_hills':'birch_forest','tall_birch_forest':'old_growth_birch_forest',
    'tall_birch_hills':'old_growth_birch_forest','wooded_mountains':'windswept_forest',
    'taiga_hills':'taiga','taiga_mountains':'taiga','snowy_taiga_hills':'snowy_taiga',
    'snowy_taiga_mountains':'snowy_taiga','giant_tree_taiga':'old_growth_pine_taiga',
    'giant_tree_taiga_hills':'old_growth_pine_taiga','giant_spruce_taiga':'old_growth_spruce_taiga',
    'giant_spruce_taiga_hills':'old_growth_spruce_taiga','jungle_hills':'jungle',
    'modified_jungle':'jungle','jungle_edge':'sparse_jungle','modified_jungle_edge':'sparse_jungle',
    'bamboo_jungle_hills':'bamboo_jungle','dark_forest_hills':'dark_forest',
    'snowy_tundra':'snowy_plains','stone_shore':'stony_shore','shattered_savanna':'windswept_savanna',
    'shattered_savanna_plateau':'windswept_savanna',
}
WOOD={
    'forest':set('forest birch_forest flower_forest old_growth_birch_forest windswept_forest dappled_forest'.split()),
    'taiga':set('taiga snowy_taiga old_growth_pine_taiga old_growth_spruce_taiga'.split()),
    'jungle':set('jungle bamboo_jungle sparse_jungle'.split()),
    'dark_pale':{'dark_forest','pale_garden'},'cherry':{'cherry_grove'},
}
FAMILIES={
    'desert':set('desert desert_hills desert_lakes'.split()),
    'swamp':set('swamp swamp_hills mangrove_swamp'.split()),
    'snowy_ice':set('snowy_plains snowy_mountains ice_spikes snowy_slopes'.split()),
    'badlands':set('badlands badlands_plateau wooded_badlands_plateau eroded_badlands modified_badlands_plateau modified_wooded_badlands_plateau wooded_badlands'.split()),
    'mushroom':{'mushroom_fields','mushroom_field_shore'},
    'plains_meadow':{'plains','sunflower_plains','meadow'},
    'mountains':set('mountains mountain_edge gravelly_mountains modified_gravelly_mountains windswept_hills windswept_gravelly_hills jagged_peaks frozen_peaks stony_peaks grove'.split()),
    'ocean':set('ocean deep_ocean warm_ocean deep_warm_ocean lukewarm_ocean deep_lukewarm_ocean cold_ocean deep_cold_ocean frozen_ocean deep_frozen_ocean'.split()),
    'rivers_shores':{'river','frozen_river','beach','stony_shore','snowy_beach'},
}
PRIMARY={name:family for family,names in WOOD.items() for name in names}
PRIMARY.update({name:family for family,names in FAMILIES.items() for name in names})
SAVANNA={'savanna','savanna_plateau','windswept_savanna'}


def canonical(name):
    name=name.removeprefix('minecraft:').lower().replace(' ','_')
    return ALIASES.get(name,name)


def classify(names, policy, complete=False, verified=False):
    names=sorted(set(canonical(n) for n in names))
    wooded=sorted(family for family,members in WOOD.items() if members.intersection(names))
    primary=sorted({PRIMARY[n] for n in names if n in PRIMARY})
    family_rewards={f:policy['family_bonuses'][f] for f in primary if f in policy['family_bonuses']}
    variant_rewards={n:v for n,v in policy['variant_bonuses'].items() if n in names}
    wood_bonus=5*max(0,len(wooded)-2)
    mapping_complete=all(n in PRIMARY or n in SAVANNA for n in names)
    status=('VERIFIED' if verified else 'PREDICTED') if len(wooded)>=2 else 'FAILED' if complete and verified and mapping_complete else 'PENDING'
    # Prediction detections are not lower bounds on actual generated coverage.
    # A verified positive sample can establish presence, but only exhaustive
    # verified coverage establishes absence of unseen families and variants.
    exhaustive=bool(complete and verified and mapping_complete)
    upper_wood=len(wooded) if exhaustive else len(WOOD)
    upper_surface=(sum(family_rewards.values())+sum(variant_rewards.values()) if exhaustive else
                   sum(policy['family_bonuses'].values())+sum(policy['variant_bonuses'].values()))
    observed_score=wood_bonus+sum(family_rewards.values())+sum(variant_rewards.values())
    upper_score=5*max(0,upper_wood-2)+upper_surface
    uncertainty={'domain':'actual_generated_surface' if verified else 'prediction_model_only',
                 'observed_wooded_family_count':len(wooded),
                 'possible_wooded_families':wooded if exhaustive else sorted(WOOD),
                 'wooded_family_count_interval':[len(wooded),upper_wood],
                 'wooded_bonus_interval':[wood_bonus,5*max(0,upper_wood-2)],
                 'surface_bonus_interval':[sum(family_rewards.values())+sum(variant_rewards.values()),upper_surface],
                 'score_interval':[observed_score,upper_score],
                 'exhaustive_verified':exhaustive,
                 'mapping_complete':mapping_complete,
                 'actual_generation_score_interval':[observed_score,upper_score] if verified else
                    [0,15+sum(policy['family_bonuses'].values())+sum(policy['variant_bonuses'].values())],
                 'interpretation':'Presence is observed; unseen families remain possible unless exhaustive verified surface coverage. Prediction intervals do not certify actual eligibility.'}
    return {'raw_names':names,'wooded_families':wooded,'wooded_family_count':len(wooded),
            'wooded_family_bitset':sum(1<<i for i,f in enumerate(WOOD) if f in wooded),
            'wooded_bonus':wood_bonus,'primary_families':primary,'family_bonuses':family_rewards,
            'variant_bonuses':variant_rewards,'surface_bonus':sum(family_rewards.values())+sum(variant_rewards.values()),
            'score':observed_score,'uncertainty':uncertainty,
            'qualification':status,'coverage':'exhaustive' if exhaustive else 'sampled or unmapped; unseen families not absent',
            'unmapped_names':[n for n in names if n not in PRIMARY and n not in SAVANNA],
            'savanna_policy':'neutral; no query, bonus, penalty or diversity credit'}


def classify_ids(ids, policy, **kwargs):
    return classify([biome_name(i) for i in sorted({int(value) for value in ids})],policy,**kwargs)


def postprocess_ids(ids, points, policy):
    """Classify unique sampled IDs and retain counts plus first observed coordinates."""
    ids=np.asarray(ids)
    if ids.ndim!=1 or len(ids)!=len(points):
        raise ValueError('Biome IDs and sample points must have equal length')
    unique,first,counts=np.unique(ids,return_index=True,return_counts=True)
    result=classify_ids(unique,policy)
    result['raw_detections']={str(int(value)):{'samples':int(count),'coordinate':points[int(index)].tolist()}
                              for value,index,count in zip(unique,first,counts)}
    return result


def variant_fit(names, variant):
    names={canonical(n) for n in names}
    # Fixed set denominator; adding Savanna cannot dilute the score.
    preferred={'forest','taiga','cherry','jungle','ocean','rivers_shores'} if variant=='v1' else {'forest','taiga','cherry','mountains','plains_meadow'}
    found={PRIMARY[n] for n in names if n in PRIMARY}
    return 100*len(found.intersection(preferred))/len(preferred)


def raster_variant_fit(raster, variant, *, target_version='26.52'):
    """Fit from actual string observations without guessing Bedrock numeric IDs.

    Sparse actual positives provide an estimate; complete verified fit requires
    every surface cell, known mapping and a one-block horizontal lattice.
    """
    metadata=raster.metadata
    observations=metadata.get('surface_biome_observations')
    if observations is not None:
        observed={};conflicts=[]
        for row in observations:
            if not isinstance(row,dict) or row.get('status')!='OBSERVED' or not isinstance(row.get('name'),str) or not row['name'].strip():
                continue
            x,z=row.get('x'),row.get('z')
            if any(isinstance(v,bool) or not isinstance(v,int) for v in (x,z)):
                continue
            if (raster.index((x,z)) is None or (x-raster.bounds[0])%raster.resolution or
                (z-raster.bounds[1])%raster.resolution):
                continue
            name=canonical(row['name'])
            if (x,z) in observed and observed[(x,z)]!=name:
                conflicts.append([x,z]);continue
            observed[(x,z)]=name
        names=sorted(set(observed.values()))
        unmapped=sorted(set(names)-set(PRIMARY)-SAVANNA)
        complete=(len(observed)==raster.height.size and not conflicts and not unmapped and
                  metadata.get('surface_biome_coverage_complete') is True)
        actual=metadata.get('source')=='actual' and metadata.get('surface_biome_evidence')=='actual_api_string_names'
        stable=metadata.get('stable_sampled_observations') is True
        status=('VERIFIED' if complete and stable and raster.resolution==1 else 'PARTIAL') if actual and names else 'UNKNOWN'
        return variant_fit(names,variant) if actual else 0.,status,{
            'source':metadata.get('surface_biome_evidence','not supplied'),'status':status,
            'raw_names':names,'unmapped_names':unmapped,'observed_cells':len(observed),
            'expected_cells':int(raster.height.size),'complete_sample_lattice':complete,
            'conflicting_coordinates':conflicts,'resolution':raster.resolution,
            'query_policy':metadata.get('surface_biome_query_policy'),'canopy_rule':metadata.get('surface_biome_canopy_rule')}
    if raster.biomes is None:
        return 0.,'UNKNOWN',{'source':'not supplied','status':'UNKNOWN'}
    names=sorted({canonical(biome_name(int(b))) for b in np.unique(raster.biomes[raster.known])})
    mapping_known=all(n in PRIMARY or n in SAVANNA for n in names)
    actual=metadata.get('source')=='actual' and metadata.get('biome_evidence')=='actual_generated'
    complete=(actual and raster.resolution==1 and raster.known.all() and mapping_known and
              metadata.get('biome_mapping_version')==target_version)
    status=('VERIFIED' if complete else 'PARTIAL') if actual and names else 'PREDICTED' if names else 'UNKNOWN'
    return variant_fit(names,variant),status,{'source':metadata.get('biome_evidence','numeric prediction IDs'),
                                          'status':status,'raw_names':names,'mapping_version':metadata.get('biome_mapping_version')}

FILE: src/seed_search/realm/bounds.py

"""Half-open block bounds; only the legacy native adapter uses inclusive maxima."""
import math
from fractions import Fraction


def square(center, side):
    if isinstance(side, bool) or not isinstance(side, int) or side <= 0 or side % 2:
        raise ValueError('Square width must be a positive even integer')
    half = side // 2
    return (center[0]-half, center[1]-half, center[0]+half, center[1]+half)


def contains(point, bounds):
    return bounds[0] <= point[0] < bounds[2] and bounds[1] <= point[1] < bounds[3]


def native_bounds(bounds):
    """Integer membership interval for an inclusive native query (also fractional bounds)."""
    lo = (math.ceil(bounds[0]), math.ceil(bounds[1]))
    hi = (math.ceil(bounds[2])-1, math.ceil(bounds[3])-1)
    if lo[0] > hi[0] or lo[1] > hi[1]:
        raise ValueError('Empty block bounds')
    return (*lo, *hi)


def nether_square(stronghold, side=896):
    return square((Fraction(int(stronghold[0]),8), Fraction(int(stronghold[1]),8)), side)


def json_bounds(bounds):
    return [int(v) if int(v) == v else float(v) for v in bounds]


def border_fit(footprint, stronghold, side=1536, complete=True):
    if footprint is None:
        return {'status':'unknown','border_fit':'unknown','footprint':None,'complete':False,
                'overflow':None,'minimum_side_blocks':None}
    border=square(stronghold,side)
    overflow={'west':max(0,border[0]-footprint[0]),'north':max(0,border[1]-footprint[1]),
              'east':max(0,footprint[2]-border[2]),'south':max(0,footprint[3]-border[3])}
    minimum=2*math.ceil(max(stronghold[0]-footprint[0],footprint[2]-stronghold[0],
                            stronghold[1]-footprint[1],footprint[3]-stronghold[1]))
    status='outside' if any(overflow.values()) else 'within' if complete else 'unknown'
    return {'status':status,'border_fit':status,'footprint':json_bounds(footprint),'complete':bool(complete),
            'overflow':overflow,'minimum_side_blocks':minimum,
            'minimum_addon_side_blocks':math.ceil(minimum/32)*32,
            'minimum_is_lower_bound':not complete,'policy_version':'local-formation-footprint-v1'}
