"""Resumable bounded lower32 ranking experiment for the fixed Realm contract."""
import fcntl
import hashlib
import heapq
import json
from pathlib import Path
import sqlite3
import time
import numpy as np

from ..backend import sweep_raw,configure_native_threads,raw_layout
from .capabilities import identity as backend_identity
from .store import canonical, RealmStore
from .resources import disk_policy,disk_status

# Derive the raw envelopes from the same fixed contract as the baseline.
# Bounds passed to native placement queries have inclusive integer maxima.
from .config import DEFAULT
from .bounds import square, nether_square, native_bounds

CENTER_BOUNDS = native_bounds(DEFAULT['discovery']['overworld_bounds'])
OW_SIDE = DEFAULT['deployment']['overworld_side_blocks']
NETHER_SIDE = DEFAULT['deployment']['nether_side_blocks']
_ow_lo = square(CENTER_BOUNDS[:2], OW_SIDE)
_ow_hi = square(CENTER_BOUNDS[2:], OW_SIDE)
_nether_lo = nether_square(CENTER_BOUNDS[:2], NETHER_SIDE)
_nether_hi = nether_square(CENTER_BOUNDS[2:], NETHER_SIDE)
OW_ENVELOPE = native_bounds((*_ow_lo[:2], *_ow_hi[2:]))
NETHER_ENVELOPE = native_bounds((*_nether_lo[:2], *_nether_hi[2:]))
FAMILIES = (('village', OW_ENVELOPE), ('ancient_city', OW_ENVELOPE),
            ('fortress', NETHER_ENVELOPE), ('bastion', NETHER_ENVELOPE),
            ('fortress', native_bounds(DEFAULT['discovery']['nether_bounds'])))


def center_rectangle(point,nether=False):
    """Inclusive integer center domain producing exact half-open membership."""
    scale,half=(8,4*NETHER_SIDE) if nether else (1,OW_SIDE//2)
    x,z=point
    bounds=(max(CENTER_BOUNDS[0],scale*x-half+1),max(CENTER_BOUNDS[1],scale*z-half+1),
            min(CENTER_BOUNDS[2],scale*x+half),min(CENTER_BOUNDS[3],scale*z+half))
    return bounds if bounds[0]<=bounds[2] and bounds[1]<=bounds[3] else None


def joint_center_witness(layouts):
    """Exact necessary raw-placement intersection for four required families.

    A witness is an allowed center, not a discovered Stronghold or viable world.
    Sweep integer X event boundaries; intersect merged inclusive Z intervals.
    """
    if len(layouts)!=4:raise ValueError('Joint layout needs Village/City/Fortress/Bastion')
    rectangles=[[r for p in points if (r:=center_rectangle(p,j>=2)) is not None] for j,points in enumerate(layouts)]
    if any(not group for group in rectangles):return None
    events=sorted({r[0] for group in rectangles for r in group}|{r[2]+1 for group in rectangles for r in group})
    def merged(intervals):
        out=[]
        for lo,hi in sorted(intervals):
            if out and lo<=out[-1][1]+1:out[-1]=(out[-1][0],max(hi,out[-1][1]))
            else:out.append((lo,hi))
        return out
    def intersect(a,b):
        i=j=0;out=[]
        while i<len(a) and j<len(b):
            lo=max(a[i][0],b[j][0]);hi=min(a[i][1],b[j][1])
            if lo<=hi:out.append((lo,hi))
            if a[i][1]<b[j][1]:i+=1
            else:j+=1
        return out
    for x in events:
        if x>CENTER_BOUNDS[2]:break
        common=[(CENTER_BOUNDS[1],CENTER_BOUNDS[3])]
        for group in rectangles:
            common=intersect(common,merged((r[1],r[3]) for r in group if r[0]<=x<=r[2]))
            if not common:break
        if common:return [x,common[0][0]]
    return None


def _score(counts):
    # Versioned ranking hint only; no permanent world rejection.
    return sum(min(int(v),32) for v in counts)


def _hash(seed,key):
    return int.from_bytes(hashlib.sha256(f'{key}:{seed}'.encode()).digest()[:8],'big')


def exploration_keys(lows,key):
    """Versioned bijective 64-bit mixing; bounded deterministic exploration."""
    values=np.asarray(lows,dtype=np.uint64)^np.uint64(_hash(0,key))
    # Vector uint64 arithmetic deliberately wraps. This changes exploration
    # policy, not raw counts or the exact legacy capped density score.
    values=values+np.uint64(0x9e3779b97f4a7c15)
    values=(values^(values>>np.uint64(30)))*np.uint64(0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9)
    values=(values^(values>>np.uint64(27)))*np.uint64(0x94d049bb133111eb)
    return values^(values>>np.uint64(31))


def run_raw(output,start,count,*,threads=1,chunk_size=256,top=64,exploration=16,key='0',resume=False,stop_after=None,mode='capped'):
    if mode not in ('reference','complete','presence','capped'):raise ValueError('Invalid raw mode')
    if (isinstance(start,bool) or not isinstance(start,int) or isinstance(count,bool) or not isinstance(count,int)
        or not 0<=start<=start+count<=2**32):raise ValueError('Raw range must fit [0,2**32)')
    for value,lo,hi in ((chunk_size,1,65536),(top,1,10000),(exploration,1,10000),(threads,1,36)):
        if isinstance(value,bool) or not isinstance(value,int) or not lo<=value<=hi:raise ValueError('Invalid raw batching/retention/thread budget')
    if stop_after is not None and (isinstance(stop_after,bool) or not isinstance(stop_after,int) or stop_after<0):raise ValueError('Invalid raw stop budget')
    root=Path(output);root.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
    ident={'version':'raw-count-rank-v4-batched','mode':mode,'exploration_policy':'splitmix64-permutation-v1','geometry':{'deployment':DEFAULT['deployment'],'discovery':DEFAULT['discovery']},'start':start,'count':count,'families':FAMILIES,
           'top':top,'exploration':exploration,'key':str(key),'backend':backend_identity()}
    ident=json.loads(canonical(ident))
    with (root/'raw.lock').open('a+') as lock:
        try:fcntl.flock(lock.fileno(),fcntl.LOCK_EX|fcntl.LOCK_NB)
        except BlockingIOError:raise ValueError('Another raw scanner owns this checkpoint') from None
        path=root/'raw.sqlite'
        if path.exists()!=resume:raise ValueError('Raw checkpoint exists; choose resume or a fresh output')
        with sqlite3.connect(path) as db:
            db.execute('PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL');db.execute('PRAGMA synchronous=FULL')
            db.executescript('CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS state(key TEXT PRIMARY KEY,value TEXT NOT NULL);'
                             'CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS chunks(start INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,end INTEGER NOT NULL,summary TEXT NOT NULL);')
            def save(k,v):db.execute('INSERT OR REPLACE INTO state VALUES (?,?)',(k,canonical(v)))
            def get(k,default=None):
                row=db.execute('SELECT value FROM state WHERE key=?',(k,)).fetchone()
                return json.loads(row[0]) if row else default
            if resume:
                if get('identity')!=ident:raise ValueError('Raw computation/selection/range identity changed')
            else:
                with db:save('identity',ident);save('next',start)
            ranked=[tuple(r) for r in get('ranked',[])];random=[tuple(r) for r in get('exploration',[])]
            heapq.heapify(ranked);heapq.heapify(random)
            at=get('next');new=0;seconds=0.;necessary_missing=get('necessary_missing',0)
            def retain(heap,item,limit):
                if len(heap)<limit:heapq.heappush(heap,item)
                elif item>heap[0]:heapq.heapreplace(heap,item)
            while at<start+count and (stop_after is None or new<stop_after):
                n=min(chunk_size,start+count-at,stop_after-new if stop_after is not None else chunk_size)
                disk_status(root,disk_policy({}),additional_bytes=(top+exploration)*2048)
                begin=time.perf_counter();values=sweep_raw(at,n,FAMILIES,threads,mode=mode);elapsed=time.perf_counter()-begin
                zeros=int((values==0).any(axis=1).sum());necessary_missing+=zeros
                scores=np.minimum(values,32).sum(axis=1)
                hashes=exploration_keys(np.arange(at,at+n,dtype=np.uint64),key)
                # Only a batch's best K can enter the global best K. Stable
                # sorting preserves the smaller-low32 tie rule and allows
                # arbitrary batch sizes across recovery without changing output.
                for offset in np.argsort(-scores,kind='stable')[:top]:
                    low=at+int(offset);v=tuple(map(int,values[offset]))
                    retain(ranked,(int(scores[offset]),-low,v),top)
                for offset in np.argsort(hashes,kind='stable')[:exploration]:
                    low=at+int(offset);v=tuple(map(int,values[offset]))
                    retain(random,(-int(hashes[offset]),-low,v),exploration)
                with db:
                    db.execute('INSERT INTO chunks VALUES (?,?,?)',(at,at+n,canonical({'inputs':n,'seconds':elapsed,'necessary_missing':zeros})))
                    save('ranked',ranked);save('exploration',random);save('next',at+n);save('necessary_missing',necessary_missing)
                at+=n;new+=n;seconds+=elapsed
            rows={}
            for label,heap in (('ranked',ranked),('exploration',random)):
                for score,negative_seed,counts in sorted(heap,reverse=True):
                    low=-negative_seed
                    rows.setdefault(low,{'low32':low,'raw_counts':list(counts),'ranking_hint':_score(counts),'selection':[]})['selection'].append(label)
            summary={'status':'COMPLETE' if at==start+count else 'PARTIAL','version':ident['version'],'range':[start,start+count],
                     'completed_lower32_inputs':at-start,'invocation_inputs':new,'invocation_raw_seconds':seconds,
                     'raw_inputs_per_second':new/seconds if seconds else None,'necessary_missing':necessary_missing,
                     'selected':sorted(rows.values(),key=lambda r:(-r['ranking_hint'],r['low32'])),
                     'policy':'shadow/ranking only; absent raw placements are not actual-world absence; joint centers not inferred',
                     'backend':ident['backend'],'threads':threads}
            summary['geometry']=ident['geometry']
            summary['exploration_policy']=ident['exploration_policy']
            summary['count_mode']=mode
            summary['count_semantics']='presence booleans' if mode=='presence' else 'min(full_count,32); saturated values are lower bounds' if mode=='capped' else 'complete bounded predictor count'
            summary['ranking_semantics']='presence-only diagnostic; not legacy density ranking' if mode=='presence' else 'legacy capped-density hint; not viability or verified yield'
            temp=root/'raw-selection.json.tmp';temp.write_text(json.dumps(summary,indent=2)+'\n');temp.replace(root/'raw-selection.json')
            return summary


def prepare_siblings(selection,high32=(0,1),limit=128):
    if isinstance(limit,bool) or not isinstance(limit,int) or not 1<=limit<=10000:raise ValueError('Sibling limit must be 1–10000')
    if len(set(high32))!=len(high32) or any(isinstance(h,bool) or not isinstance(h,int) or not 0<=h<2**32 for h in high32):raise ValueError('High32 siblings must be distinct uint32 values')
    seeds=[]
    for row in selection['selected']:
        for high in high32:
            unsigned=(high<<32)|row['low32'];seed=unsigned if unsigned<2**63 else unsigned-2**64
            seeds.append({'seed':str(seed),'low32':row['low32'],'high32':high,'selection':row['selection']})
            if len(seeds)>=limit:return seeds
    return seeds


def dispersed_high32(count=4,key='0'):
    if isinstance(count,bool) or not isinstance(count,int) or not 1<=count<=65536:
        raise ValueError('Sibling count must be 1–65536')
    offset=_hash(0,key)&0xffffffff
    # Odd multiplier is a permutation modulo 2**32; prefixes are dispersed.
    return tuple((offset+i*0x9e3779b1)&0xffffffff for i in range(count))


def nether_center_coverage(layouts):
    """Exact integer feasible-center area, shared by both required Nether types."""
    if len(layouts)!=2:raise ValueError('Need Fortress and Bastion layouts')
    groups=[[r for p in points if (r:=center_rectangle(p,True)) is not None] for points in layouts]
    if any(not g for g in groups):return {'area':0,'fraction':0.,'witness':None,'regions':[]}
    events=sorted({r[0] for g in groups for r in g}|{r[2]+1 for g in groups for r in g})
    regions=[];area=0
    for x,next_x in zip(events,events[1:]):
        intersections=[]
        for a in groups[0]:
            if not a[0]<=x<=a[2]:continue
            for b in groups[1]:
                if b[0]<=x<=b[2]:
                    lo,hi=max(a[1],b[1]),min(a[3],b[3])
                    if lo<=hi:intersections.append((lo,hi))
        merged=[]
        for lo,hi in sorted(intersections):
            if merged and lo<=merged[-1][1]+1:merged[-1]=(merged[-1][0],max(hi,merged[-1][1]))
            else:merged.append((lo,hi))
        for lo,hi in merged:
            regions.append([x,lo,next_x-1,hi]);area+=(next_x-x)*(hi-lo+1)
    total=(CENTER_BOUNDS[2]-CENTER_BOUNDS[0]+1)*(CENTER_BOUNDS[3]-CENTER_BOUNDS[1]+1)
    return {'area':area,'fraction':area/total,'witness':regions[0][:2] if regions else None,'regions':regions}


def rank_geometry(selection, *, top=64, exploration=16, key='0'):
    """Re-rank a bounded raw pool; feature overhead is explicit and charged."""
    if len(selection.get('selected',[]))>10000 or top<1 or exploration<1:
        raise ValueError('Geometry ranking needs a bounded pool and positive retention')
    if selection.get('geometry')!={'deployment':DEFAULT['deployment'],'discovery':DEFAULT['discovery']}:
        raise ValueError('Geometry selection contract changed')
    if selection.get('backend')!=backend_identity():raise ValueError('Geometry ranking backend changed')
    begin=time.perf_counter();cpu=time.process_time();rows=[]
    for row in selection['selected']:
        low=row['low32']
        coverage=nether_center_coverage([raw_layout(low,name,NETHER_ENVELOPE) for name in ('fortress','bastion')])
        rows.append({**row,'geometry_hint':coverage,'ranking_hint':coverage['fraction'],'selection':[]})
    ranked=sorted(rows,key=lambda r:(-r['ranking_hint'],r['low32']))[:top]
    random=sorted(rows,key=lambda r:_hash(r['low32'],key))[:exploration]
    retained={}
    for label,group in (('geometry',ranked),('exploration',random)):
        for row in group:
            retained.setdefault(row['low32'],row)['selection'].append(label)
    return {**selection,'selected':sorted(retained.values(),key=lambda r:(-r['ranking_hint'],r['low32'])),
            'ranking_policy':'nether-feasible-center-area-v1','feature_pool_inputs':len(rows),
            'feature_seconds':time.perf_counter()-begin,'feature_cpu_seconds':time.process_time()-cpu,
            'ranking_semantics':'bounded geometric ranking experiment; full64 viability and actual Stronghold centers unresolved'}


def evaluate_siblings(cfg,output,selection,*,high32=(0,1),limit=128,resume=False):
    """Finite full64 handoff with durable per-input baseline/anchor evidence."""
    from .baseline import evaluate_seed
    expected={'deployment':cfg['deployment'],'discovery':cfg['discovery']}
    if selection.get('geometry')!=expected:raise ValueError('Raw selection geometry differs from full64 contract')
    if selection.get('backend')!=backend_identity():raise ValueError('Raw selection backend identity changed')
    configure_native_threads(cfg['search'].get('native_threads_per_worker',1))
    inputs=prepare_siblings(selection,high32,limit)
    import copy
    from .runner import run_scan
    cfg=copy.deepcopy(cfg);cfg['search'].update(sequence='manifest',manifest_seeds=[r['seed'] for r in inputs])
    root=Path(output)
    if not root.exists():
        from .capabilities import report
        root.mkdir(parents=True)
        (root/'compatibility.json').write_text(json.dumps(report(cfg),indent=2)+'\n')
    identity={'policy':'raw-full64-siblings-v2','config':cfg,'backend':backend_identity(),'inputs':inputs}
    run_scan(cfg,output,len(inputs),workers=cfg['search']['workers'],resume=resume,identity=identity,allow_prediction_scale=True)
    with RealmStore(output,resume=True) as store:
        store.set_state('raw_selection_provenance',{'inputs':inputs,'raw_range':selection['range'],'bias':'finite selected lower32/high32 siblings; not exhaustive'})
        return store.status()


if __name__=='__main__':
    import argparse
    parser=argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    commands=parser.add_subparsers(dest='command',required=True)
    scan=commands.add_parser('scan');scan.add_argument('--output',required=True);scan.add_argument('--start',type=int,default=0)
    scan.add_argument('--count',type=int,required=True);scan.add_argument('--threads',type=int,default=1)
    scan.add_argument('--top',type=int,default=64);scan.add_argument('--exploration',type=int,default=16)
    scan.add_argument('--chunk-size',type=int,default=256);scan.add_argument('--resume',action='store_true')
    sibling=commands.add_parser('siblings');sibling.add_argument('--selection',required=True);sibling.add_argument('--output',required=True)
    sibling.add_argument('--config');sibling.add_argument('--limit',type=int,default=128);sibling.add_argument('--high32',type=int,nargs='+',default=[0,1]);sibling.add_argument('--resume',action='store_true')
    args=parser.parse_args()
    if args.command=='scan':result=run_raw(args.output,args.start,args.count,threads=args.threads,top=args.top,exploration=args.exploration,chunk_size=args.chunk_size,resume=args.resume)
    else:
        from .cli import load
        result=evaluate_siblings(load(args.config),args.output,json.loads(Path(args.selection).read_text()),high32=args.high32,limit=args.limit,resume=args.resume)
    print(json.dumps(result,indent=2))


========================================================================
FILE: src/seed_search/realm/siblings.py
========================================================================
"""Bounded adaptive sibling allocation; statistical selection never proves absence."""
import copy
import fcntl
import hashlib
import json
import math
from pathlib import Path
import time

from ..staged import compose_seed
from .raw import dispersed_high32
from .runner import run_scan
from .store import RealmStore
from .capabilities import identity as backend_identity


def allocation_members(layouts, stats, limit, key, exploration_fraction=.2):
    """Promote observed yield with a deterministic uniform exploration reserve."""
    if not 0 <= exploration_fraction <= 1:
        raise ValueError('Invalid exploration fraction')
    def rank(low):
        row=stats[str(low)]
        # A smoothed rate avoids promoting a layout solely on one fortunate
        # sibling. This is a ranking hint, never a full64 viability veto.
        rate=(row['qualified']+.5)/(row['evaluated']+1)
        return (-rate,-row['anchors'],row['evaluated'],low)
    exploration=math.ceil(limit*exploration_fraction)
    random=sorted(layouts,key=lambda low:hashlib.sha256(f'{key}:{low}'.encode()).digest())[:exploration]
    remaining=[low for low in sorted(layouts,key=rank) if low not in set(random)]
    return random+remaining[:max(0,limit-len(random))]


def run_adaptive(cfg, output, layouts, *, budget=4096, key='0', resume=False, workers=None,
                 promotion=(256,128,64), progress=None):
    """Recoverable rounds 1/4/16; total inputs may be below the maximum budget."""
    if not layouts or len(layouts)>256 or len(set(layouts))!=len(layouts) or any(
            isinstance(low,bool) or not isinstance(low,int) or not 0<=low<2**32 for low in layouts):
        raise ValueError('Adaptive search requires 1–256 distinct lower32 layouts')
    if isinstance(budget,bool) or not isinstance(budget,int) or not 1<=budget<=10000:
        raise ValueError('Adaptive budget must be 1–10000 full64 inputs')
    if len(promotion)!=3 or any(isinstance(n,bool) or not isinstance(n,int) or n<1 or n>256 for n in promotion):
        raise ValueError('Invalid promotion counts')
    root=Path(output);root.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
    lock=(root/'adaptive.lock').open('a+')
    try:
        try:fcntl.flock(lock.fileno(),fcntl.LOCK_EX|fcntl.LOCK_NB)
        except BlockingIOError:raise ValueError('Another adaptive allocator owns this checkpoint') from None
        identity={'policy':'adaptive-siblings-v1','config':cfg,'backend':backend_identity(),
                  'layouts':layouts,'budget':budget,'key':str(key),'promotion':list(promotion)}
        with RealmStore(root,identity,resume) as parent:
            stats=parent.get_state('adaptive_stats') or {str(low):{'evaluated':0,'qualified':0,'anchors':0} for low in layouts}
            next_round=parent.get_state('adaptive_next_round') or 0
            used=sum(r['evaluated'] for r in stats.values())
            highs=dispersed_high32(16,key)
            records=parent.get_state('adaptive_rounds') or []
            for number,target in enumerate((1,4,16)):
                if number<next_round or used>=budget:continue
                chosen=layouts if number==0 else allocation_members(layouts,stats,min(len(layouts),promotion[number]),f'{key}:round:{number}')
                rows=[]
                # Round-robin siblings prevents an exhausted budget favoring the
                # first selected layout with many more upper32 evaluations.
                for sibling in range(target):
                    for low in chosen:
                        if sibling>=stats[str(low)]['evaluated'] and len(rows)<budget-used:
                            rows.append({'low32':low,'high32':highs[sibling],
                                         'seed':str(compose_seed(low,highs[sibling])),'round':number})
                if not rows:continue
                run_cfg=copy.deepcopy(cfg)
                run_cfg['search'].update(sequence='manifest',manifest_seeds=[r['seed'] for r in rows])
                child=root/f'round-{number}'
                if not child.exists():
                    from .capabilities import report
                    child.mkdir(parents=True)
                    (child/'compatibility.json').write_text(json.dumps(report(run_cfg),indent=2)+'\n')
                begin=time.perf_counter();cpu=time.process_time()
                child_identity={'policy':'adaptive-round-v1','config':run_cfg,'backend':identity['backend'],'inputs':rows}
                status=run_scan(run_cfg,child,len(rows),workers=workers or cfg['search']['workers'],
                                resume=(child/'realm.sqlite').exists(),identity=child_identity,allow_prediction_scale=True)
                elapsed=time.perf_counter()-begin;parent_cpu=time.process_time()-cpu
                with RealmStore(child,resume=True) as store:
                    for row,result in zip(rows,store.iter_results()):
                        if result['seed']!=row['seed']:raise ValueError('Adaptive sibling manifest mismatch')
                        record=stats[str(row['low32'])]
                        qualified=sum(a['qualification']=='PREDICTED' for a in result['anchors'])
                        record['evaluated']+=1;record['qualified']+=int(qualified>0);record['anchors']+=qualified
                used+=len(rows)
                record={'round':number,'target_siblings':target,'admitted_layouts':len(chosen),'new_inputs':len(rows),
                        'cumulative_inputs':used,'seconds':elapsed,'parent_cpu_seconds':parent_cpu,
                        'worker_cpu_seconds':status['worker_statistics']['worker_cpu_seconds'],
                        'qualified_seeds':sum(r['qualified'] for r in stats.values()),
                        'qualifying_anchors':sum(r['anchors'] for r in stats.values()),'run':str(child)}
                records.append(record)
                with parent.db:
                    parent._set('adaptive_stats',stats);parent._set('adaptive_next_round',number+1);parent._set('adaptive_rounds',records)
                if progress:progress(record)
            summary={'policy':identity['policy'],'budget':budget,'evaluated_inputs':used,'rounds':records,
                     'qualified_seeds':sum(r['qualified'] for r in stats.values()),
                     'qualifying_anchors':sum(r['anchors'] for r in stats.values()),'stats':stats,
                     'selection_semantics':'finite heuristic allocation with 20% uniform exploration; no lossless world rejection'}
            parent.set_state('adaptive_summary',summary)
            return summary
    finally:lock.close()


========================================================================
FILE: src/seed_search/backend.py

from __future__ import annotations

import threading
import time
from functools import wraps
from collections import OrderedDict
import numpy as np
from .seeds import parse_seed, MASK64
from ._native import ffi, lib

STRUCTURES = ("trial_chamber", "stronghold", "mineshaft", "ancient_city", "village",
              "ruined_portal", "shipwreck", "monument", "outpost", "fortress", "bastion",
              "desert_pyramid", "jungle_temple", "swamp_hut", "igloo", "ocean_ruin", "mansion",
              "buried_treasure", "trail_ruins", "abandoned_camp", "ruined_portal_nether", "end_city")
# Serializes wrapper access in a Python process; multiprocessing remains parallel.
_LOCK = threading.RLock()
# These pinned-version families use a uint32_t MT placement seed. Stronghold's
# village path, Mineshaft and Trial Chamber are deliberately excluded.
RAW_MT_TYPES=frozenset(('village','ancient_city','ruined_portal','shipwreck','monument','outpost','fortress','bastion'))
_RAW_LAYOUTS=OrderedDict()
_NATIVE_THREADS=1

def native_runtime():
    import hashlib
    from pathlib import Path
    ptr=lib.ss_openmp_library()
    path=Path(ffi.string(ptr).decode()) if ptr!=ffi.NULL else None
    return {'openmp':bool(lib.ss_openmp_enabled()),
            'runtime_sha256':hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest() if path else None,
            'policy':'outer-columns-static-min256-v1'}

def configure_native_threads(threads=1):
    """Set defaults for new handles; numerical-library thread limits stay separate."""
    global _NATIVE_THREADS
    if isinstance(threads,bool) or not isinstance(threads,int) or not 1<=threads<=36:
        raise ValueError('Native threads must be 1–36')
    with _LOCK:
        if threads>1 and not lib.ss_openmp_enabled():
            raise ValueError('Native threading requires an explicitly built OpenMP runtime')
        _NATIVE_THREADS=threads


def measure(operation,points=None):
    """Inclusive API timing; counters describe executed work, not eligibility."""
    def decorate(fn):
        @wraps(fn)
        def wrapped(self,*args,**kwargs):
            t=time.perf_counter()
            try:return fn(self,*args,**kwargs)
            finally:
                row=self.native_work.setdefault(operation,{'calls':0,'points':0,'seconds':0.})
                row['calls']+=1;row['seconds']+=time.perf_counter()-t
                if points is not None:
                    try:row['points']+=points(args,kwargs)
                    except (IndexError,TypeError,ValueError):pass
        return wrapped
    return decorate

def _int(value: int) -> int:
    if not isinstance(value, (int, np.integer)) or isinstance(value, bool):
        raise ValueError("Coordinates and sizes must be integers")
    value = int(value)
    if not -(1 << 30) <= value < (1 << 30):
        raise ValueError("Coordinate outside safe prototype range")
    return value

def _buffer(array, ctype):
    return ffi.from_buffer(ctype, array)

def biome_name(biome: int) -> str:
    ptr = lib.ss_biome_name(int(biome))
    return ffi.string(ptr).decode() if ptr != ffi.NULL else f"unknown_{biome}"


def biome_catalog():
    """Distinguish version-active IDs from legacy names and internal End classes."""
    result=[]
    for value in range(256):
        dimension=ffi.new('int[1]');generates=ffi.new('int[1]')
        exists=lib.ss_biome_catalog(value,dimension,generates)
        name=biome_name(value)
        if exists or not name.startswith('unknown_'):
            result.append({'id':value,'name':name,'version_active':bool(exists),
                           'generates':bool(generates[0]),'dimension':int(dimension[0]),
                           'provenance':'pinned predictor ID; not an actual Bedrock string mapping'})
    return result

def structure_type(name: str) -> int:
    if name not in STRUCTURES:
        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported structure: {name}")
    return int(lib.ss_structure_type(name.encode()))

def sweep_raw(start,count,families,threads=1,mode="reference"):
    modes={"reference":0,"presence":1,"capped":2,"complete":3}
    if mode not in modes:raise ValueError("Unknown raw count mode")
    """Bounded generator-free raw placement counts; never full64 eligibility."""
    if (isinstance(start,bool) or not isinstance(start,int) or not 0<=start<=2**32 or
        isinstance(count,bool) or not isinstance(count,int) or not 0<=count<=65536 or start+count>2**32):
        raise ValueError('Raw range must fit lower32 and contain at most 65536 inputs')
    if isinstance(threads,bool) or not isinstance(threads,int) or not 1<=threads<=36:
        raise ValueError('Invalid raw native thread count')
    if not 1<=len(families)<=8 or any(name not in RAW_MT_TYPES for name,bounds in families):
        raise ValueError('Raw sweep supports 1–8 reviewed MT families')
    types=np.array([structure_type(name) for name,bounds in families],np.int32)
    bounds=np.array([[ _int(v) for v in b] for name,b in families],np.int32)
    if bounds.shape!=(len(families),4):raise ValueError('Raw bounds need four inclusive coordinates')
    out=np.empty((count,len(families)),np.int32)
    with _LOCK:
        status=lib.ss_sweep_raw_mode(start,count,_buffer(types,'int[]'),_buffer(bounds,'int[]'),len(families),threads,
                                _buffer(out,'int[]'),out.size,modes[mode])
    if status!=0:raise ValueError('Raw sweep invalid runtime, regions or output capacity')
    return out

class Backend:
    def __init__(self, seed: str | int, raw_cache_entries: int = 0):
        self.native_work={}
        self.seed = parse_seed(seed)
        if isinstance(raw_cache_entries,bool) or not isinstance(raw_cache_entries,int) or not 0<=raw_cache_entries<=256:
            raise ValueError('Raw layout cache must contain 0–256 entries')
        self.raw_cache_entries=raw_cache_entries
        with _LOCK:
            ptr = lib.ss_create(self.seed & MASK64)
            if ptr != ffi.NULL and _NATIVE_THREADS>1:
                if lib.ss_set_threads(ptr,_NATIVE_THREADS)!=0:
                    lib.ss_destroy(ptr)
                    raise ValueError('Native thread configuration unavailable')
        if ptr == ffi.NULL:
            raise MemoryError("Could not allocate generator")
        self._ctx = ffi.gc(ptr, lib.ss_destroy)

    def close(self):
        with _LOCK:
            if self._ctx is not None:
                ffi.release(self._ctx)
                self._ctx = None

    def __enter__(self):
        return self

    def __exit__(self, *_):
        self.close()

    def _handle(self):
        if self._ctx is None:
            raise RuntimeError("Backend is closed")
        return self._ctx

    def biome(self, x: int, y: int, z: int, dimension: int = 0) -> int:
        if dimension not in (0, -1, 1):
            raise ValueError("Supported dimensions are Overworld, Nether and End")
        with _LOCK:
            return int(lib.ss_biome(self._handle(), dimension, _int(x), _int(y), _int(z)))

    @measure('grid',lambda a,k:int(a[2])*int(a[3]))
    def grid(self, x0: int, z0: int, nx: int, nz: int, step: int = 64, y: int = 63):
        x0,z0,nx,nz,step,y = map(_int,(x0,z0,nx,nz,step,y))
        if nx < 1 or nz < 1 or step < 1 or nx*nz > 100_000:
            raise ValueError("Grid must contain 1–100,000 points with positive spacing")
        _int(x0+(nx-1)*step); _int(z0+(nz-1)*step)
        biomes=np.empty((nz,nx),dtype=np.int32)
        climate=np.empty((nz,nx,6),dtype=np.float64)
        approx=np.empty((nz,nx),dtype=np.float64)
        with _LOCK:
            code=lib.ss_grid(self._handle(),x0,z0,nx,nz,step,y,
                             _buffer(biomes,"int[]"),_buffer(climate,"double[]"),_buffer(approx,"double[]"))
        if code:
            raise RuntimeError(f"Native grid error {code}")
        return {"biomes":biomes,"climate":climate,"approx_height":approx}

    @measure('biome_points',lambda a,k:len(a[0]))
    def biome_points(self, xyz, dimension=0):
        points=np.asarray(xyz)
        if dimension not in (0,-1,1) or points.ndim!=2 or points.shape[1]!=3 or len(points)>100000 or not np.issubdtype(points.dtype,np.integer):
            raise ValueError('Expected up to 100,000 integer X/Y/Z points in a supported dimension')
        if np.any(points<-(1<<30)) or np.any(points>=(1<<30)):raise ValueError('Point outside safe range')
        points=np.ascontiguousarray(points,dtype=np.int32);out=np.empty(len(points),dtype=np.int32)
        with _LOCK:
            code=lib.ss_biome_points(self._handle(),dimension,_buffer(points,'int[]'),len(points),_buffer(out,'int[]'))
        if code:raise RuntimeError(f'Native biome error {code}')
        return out

    @measure('heights',lambda a,k:len(a[0]))
    def heights(self, xz, vertical_step: int = 8):
        points=np.asarray(xz)
        if points.ndim != 2 or points.shape[1] != 2 or len(points)>100_000:
            raise ValueError("Expected up to 100,000 X/Z coordinate pairs")
        if not np.issubdtype(points.dtype,np.integer):
            raise ValueError("Point coordinates must be integers")
        if np.any(points < -(1<<30)) or np.any(points >= (1<<30)):
            raise ValueError("Point outside safe prototype range")
        points=np.ascontiguousarray(points,dtype=np.int32)
        if not isinstance(vertical_step,int) or not 1<=vertical_step<=16:
            raise ValueError("Vertical step must be an integer in 1–16")
        heights=np.empty(len(points),dtype=np.int32)
        biomes=np.empty(len(points),dtype=np.int32)
        with _LOCK:
            code=lib.ss_heights(self._handle(),_buffer(points,"int[]"),len(points),vertical_step,
                               _buffer(heights,"int[]"),_buffer(biomes,"int[]"))
        if code:
            raise RuntimeError(f"Native height error {code}")
        return heights,biomes

    @measure('raw_land',lambda a,k:len(a[0]))
    def land(self, xz):
        points=np.asarray(xz)
        if points.ndim!=2 or points.shape[1]!=2 or len(points)>100_000 or not np.issubdtype(points.dtype,np.integer):
            raise ValueError('Expected up to 100,000 integer X/Z pairs')
        if np.any(points<-(1<<30)) or np.any(points>=(1<<30)):raise ValueError('Point outside safe range')
        points=np.ascontiguousarray(points,dtype=np.int32);out=np.empty(len(points),dtype=np.int32)
        with _LOCK:
            code=lib.ss_land(self._handle(),_buffer(points,'int[]'),len(points),_buffer(out,'int[]'))
        if code:raise RuntimeError(f'Native land classification error {code}')
        return out.astype(bool)

    @measure('placement_and_viability')
    def structures(self, name: str, bounds: tuple[int,int,int,int], viable: bool = True):
        typ=structure_type(name)
        x0,z0,x1,z1=map(_int,bounds)
        if x0>x1 or z0>z1 or x1-x0>14_000 or z1-z0>14_000:
            raise ValueError("Invalid or oversized prototype structure bounds")
        cap=((x1-x0)//16+3)*((z1-z0)//16+3)+20
        out=np.empty((cap,2),dtype=np.int32)
        with _LOCK:
            n=lib.ss_scan(self._handle(),typ,x0,z0,x1,z1,int(viable),_buffer(out,"int[]"),cap)
        if n<0:
            raise RuntimeError(f"Native structure scan error {n} for {name}")
        return sorted({(int(x),int(z)) for x,z in out[:n]})

    def attempt(self,name: str,rx: int,rz: int):
        out=ffi.new("int[2]")
        with _LOCK:
            result=lib.ss_attempt(self._handle(),structure_type(name),_int(rx),_int(rz),out)
        if result<0:
            raise ValueError("This type requires its special scan adapter")
        return (int(out[0]),int(out[1])) if result else None

    @measure('mineshaft_first')
    def mineshaft_first(self,bounds):
        self._handle()
        x0,z0,x1,z1=map(_int,bounds)
        if x0>x1 or z0>z1 or x1-x0>14000 or z1-z0>14000:
            raise ValueError('Invalid or oversized Mineshaft bounds')
        out=ffi.new('int[2]')
        with _LOCK:
            code=lib.ss_mineshaft_first(self._handle(),x0,z0,x1,z1,out)
        if code<0:raise RuntimeError(f'Native Mineshaft existence error {code}')
        return (int(out[0]),int(out[1])) if code else None

    @measure('nether_pair')
    def nether_structure_pairs(self,bounds):
        self._handle()
        x0,z0,x1,z1=map(_int,bounds)
        if x0>x1 or z0>z1 or x1-x0>14000 or z1-z0>14000:
            raise ValueError('Invalid or oversized Nether bounds')
        cap=((x1-x0)//16+3)*((z1-z0)//16+3)+20
        pts=np.empty((cap,3),np.int32);flags=np.empty(cap,np.int32)
        with _LOCK:
            n=lib.ss_nether_pair(self._handle(),x0,z0,x1,z1,_buffer(pts,'int[]'),_buffer(flags,'int[]'),cap)
        if n<0:raise RuntimeError(f'Native Nether pair error {n}')
        result={name:([],[]) for name in ('fortress','bastion')}
        for (x,z,fortress),ok in zip(pts[:n],flags[:n]):
            raw,viable=result['fortress' if fortress else 'bastion']
            point=(int(x),int(z));raw.append(point)
            if ok:viable.append(point)
        return result

    @measure('structure_first')
    def structure_first(self,name,bounds):
        self._handle();typ=structure_type(name)
        x0,z0,x1,z1=map(_int,bounds)
        if x0>x1 or z0>z1 or x1-x0>14000 or z1-z0>14000:
            raise ValueError('Invalid or oversized structure bounds')
        if name=='stronghold':raise ValueError('Stronghold discovery must enumerate its anchors')
        out=ffi.new('int[2]')
        with _LOCK:
            code=lib.ss_structure_first(self._handle(),typ,x0,z0,x1,z1,out)
        if code<0:raise RuntimeError(f'Native structure witness error {code}')
        return (int(out[0]),int(out[1])) if code else None

    @measure('combined_structure_scan')
    def structure_pair(self,name,bounds):
        """One placement scan, then full-seed viability. Cache ONLY raw MT layouts."""
        # Validate before a cache hit, including a closed handle and bounds.
        self._handle();typ=structure_type(name);bounds=tuple(map(_int,bounds))
        x0,z0,x1,z1=bounds
        if x0>x1 or z0>z1 or x1-x0>14000 or z1-z0>14000:
            raise ValueError('Invalid or oversized prototype structure bounds')
        key=(name,self.seed & 0xffffffff,bounds) if name in RAW_MT_TYPES and self.raw_cache_entries else None
        with _LOCK:
            raw=_RAW_LAYOUTS.pop(key,None) if key is not None else None
            if raw is None:
                raw=tuple(self.structures(name,bounds,False))
            if key is not None:
                _RAW_LAYOUTS[key]=raw
                while len(_RAW_LAYOUTS)>self.raw_cache_entries:_RAW_LAYOUTS.popitem(last=False)
            if not raw:return [],[]
            pts=np.ascontiguousarray(raw,dtype=np.int32)
            flags=np.empty(len(raw),dtype=np.int32)
            code=lib.ss_filter_starts(self._handle(),typ,_buffer(pts,'int[]'),len(raw),_buffer(flags,'int[]'))
            if code:raise RuntimeError(f'Native start filter error {code}')
            return list(raw),[p for p,ok in zip(raw,flags) if ok]

def model_metadata():
    return {"model": ffi.string(lib.ss_model_name()).decode(),
            "structure_rng": {name: int(lib.ss_structure_rng(structure_type(name))) for name in STRUCTURES}}

def capabilities():
    return {'edition':'bedrock','target_version':'26.52','model':'MC_26_50',
            'mapping_status':'bounded_matching_version_Chunkbase_comparison',
            'dimensions':['overworld','nether','end'],'structures':list(STRUCTURES),
            'biome_inventory':biome_catalog(),
            'terrain':'raw density estimate, no caves/aquifers/decoration/chunk interpolation',
            'woodland':'surface biome proxy, no tree counts',
            'unsupported':['actual structure footprints','portal arrival','spawn','trees','End terrain and actual Bedrock End biome mapping','monster_room bounded adapter'],
            'lower_bit_optimization':'bounded_lower32_staged_eight_raw_families',
            'quart_land_reuse':'pinned_raw_density_quart_xz_v1'}

def raw_layout(low32,name,bounds):
    if isinstance(low32,bool) or not isinstance(low32,int) or not 0<=low32<2**32:
        raise ValueError('low32 must be an unsigned 32-bit integer')
    if name not in RAW_MT_TYPES:raise ValueError('Raw layout adapter accepts only the eight verified MT families')
    x0,z0,x1,z1=map(_int,bounds)
    if x0>x1 or z0>z1 or x1-x0>14000 or z1-z0>14000:raise ValueError('Invalid or oversized structure bounds')
    cap=((x1-x0)//16+3)*((z1-z0)//16+3)+20;out=np.empty((cap,2),np.int32)
    with _LOCK:
        n=lib.ss_raw_layout(low32,structure_type(name),x0,z0,x1,z1,_buffer(out,'int[]'),cap)
    if n<0:raise RuntimeError(f'Native raw layout error {n}')
    return sorted({(int(x),int(z)) for x,z in out[:n]})

def structure_diagnostic(backend, name, x, z):
    out=ffi.new('int[10]')
    with _LOCK:
        ok=lib.ss_diagnostic(backend._handle(),structure_type(name),_int(x),_int(z),out)
    if not ok: raise ValueError('Diagnostic supports Bastion and Ancient City only')
    return {'quart_sample':[int(out[i]) for i in range(3)],'sample_biome':biome_name(out[3]),
            'rotation':int(out[4]),'variant':int(out[5]),'piece_offset':[int(out[6]),int(out[7])],
            'piece_size':[int(out[8]),int(out[9])],'start_biome':biome_name(backend.biome(x,-27 if name=='ancient_city' else 33,z,0 if name=='ancient_city' else -1))}

#include "bridge.h"
#include "generator.h"
#include "finders.h"
#include "cave.h"
#include "util.h"
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <limits.h>
#ifdef _OPENMP
#include <omp.h>
#endif

/* The opaque handle owns all generator/spline buffers. Never copy it. */
struct SeedContext {
    uint64_t seed;
    Generator overworld, nether, end;
    int end_ready;
    TerrainNoise terrain;
    TerrainShaper shaper;
    int terrain_ready;
    int threads;
};

/* Pinned raw model: the expensive noise term is constant within each eight-Y
 * cell. Preserve the exact scalar expression and all Y decisions; this is not
 * interpolation or a coarse vertical scan. No state crosses columns/seeds. */
typedef struct DensityCell { int y; double noise; } DensityCell;
static double density_cell(const TerrainNoise *terrain, double offset, double factor,
                           int x, int y, int z, DensityCell *cell) {
    int m=floordiv(y,8);
    if (cell->y!=m) {
        cell->noise=sampleSurfaceNoise(&terrain->sn,floordiv(x,4),m,floordiv(z,4))/128.0;
        cell->y=m;
    }
    double g=(1.0-(double)y/128.0)+offset;
    double e=g*factor;
    e *= (e>0.0) ? 4.0 : 1.0;
    double result=e+cell->noise;
    if(result>64.0)result=64.0;
    if(result<-64.0)result=-64.0;
    int yc=m+8;
    result=clampedLerp(((double)(48-yc)-8.0)/2.0,-0.0781250,result);
    result=clampedLerp((double)yc/3.0,0.1171875,result);
    if(result>64.0)result=64.0;
    if(result<-64.0)result=-64.0;
    return result;
}

SeedContext *ss_create(uint64_t seed) {
    SeedContext *ctx = calloc(1, sizeof(*ctx));
    if (!ctx) return NULL;
    ctx->seed = seed;
    ctx->threads = 1;
    setupGenerator(&ctx->overworld, MC_26_50, 0);
    setupGenerator(&ctx->nether, MC_26_50, 0);
    applySeed(&ctx->overworld, DIM_OVERWORLD, seed);
    applySeed(&ctx->nether, DIM_NETHER, seed);
    return ctx;
}
void ss_destroy(SeedContext *ctx) { free(ctx); }
int ss_openmp_enabled(void) {
#ifdef _OPENMP
    return 1;
#else
    return 0;
#endif
}
const char *ss_openmp_library(void) {
#ifdef SS_OPENMP_LIBRARY
    return SS_OPENMP_LIBRARY;
#else
    return NULL;
#endif
}
int ss_set_threads(SeedContext *ctx,int threads) {
    if(!ctx || threads<1 || threads>36 || (threads>1 && !ss_openmp_enabled()))return -1;
    ctx->threads=threads;
    return 0;
}
int ss_model(void) { return MC_26_50; }
const char *ss_model_name(void) { return mc2str(MC_26_50); }
const char *ss_biome_name(int id) {
    /* Pinned util.c omits these existing enum entries. */
    switch (id) {
        case small_end_islands: return "small_end_islands";
        case end_midlands: return "end_midlands";
        case end_highlands: return "end_highlands";
        case end_barrens: return "end_barrens";
        case grove: return "grove";
        case snowy_slopes: return "snowy_slopes";
        case jagged_peaks: return "jagged_peaks";
        case frozen_peaks: return "frozen_peaks";
        default: return biome2str(MC_26_50,id);
    }
}
int ss_biome_catalog(int id,int *dimension,int *generates) {
    if(!dimension || !generates || id<0 || id>255)return -1;
    *dimension=getDimension(id);
    *generates=(*dimension==DIM_OVERWORLD)?isOverworld(MC_26_50,id):biomeExists(MC_26_50,id);
    return biomeExists(MC_26_50,id);
}
int ss_structure_type(const char *name) {
    if (!strcmp(name,"desert_pyramid")) return Desert_Pyramid;
    if (!strcmp(name,"jungle_temple")) return Jungle_Pyramid;
    if (!strcmp(name,"swamp_hut")) return Swamp_Hut;
    if (!strcmp(name,"igloo")) return Igloo;
    if (!strcmp(name,"ocean_ruin")) return Ocean_Ruin;
    if (!strcmp(name,"mansion")) return Mansion;
    if (!strcmp(name,"buried_treasure")) return Treasure;
    if (!strcmp(name,"trail_ruins")) return Trail_Ruins;
    if (!strcmp(name,"abandoned_camp")) return Abandoned_Camp;
    if (!strcmp(name,"ruined_portal_nether")) return Ruined_Portal_N;
    if (!strcmp(name,"end_city")) return End_City;
    if (!strcmp(name,"trial_chamber")) return Trial_Chambers;
    if (!strcmp(name,"stronghold")) return Stronghold;
    if (!strcmp(name,"mineshaft")) return Mineshaft;
    if (!strcmp(name,"ancient_city")) return Ancient_City;
    if (!strcmp(name,"village")) return Village;
    if (!strcmp(name,"ruined_portal")) return Ruined_Portal;
    if (!strcmp(name,"shipwreck")) return Shipwreck;
    if (!strcmp(name,"monument")) return Monument;
    if (!strcmp(name,"outpost")) return Outpost;
    if (!strcmp(name,"fortress")) return Fortress;
    if (!strcmp(name,"bastion")) return Bastion;
    return -1;
}
int ss_structure_rng(int type) {
    StructureConfig config;
    return getStructureConfig(type, MC_26_50, &config) ? config.rngType : -1;
}
static Generator *ss_generator(SeedContext *ctx, int dimension) {
    if (!ctx) return NULL;
    if (dimension == DIM_OVERWORLD) return &ctx->overworld;
    if (dimension == DIM_NETHER) return &ctx->nether;
    if (dimension != DIM_END) return NULL;
    if (!ctx->end_ready) {
        setupGenerator(&ctx->end, MC_26_50, 0);
        applySeed(&ctx->end, DIM_END, ctx->seed);
        ctx->end_ready=1;
    }
    return &ctx->end;
}
int ss_biome(SeedContext *ctx, int dimension, int x, int y, int z) {
    Generator *g=ss_generator(ctx, dimension);
    if (!g) return none;
    if (dimension==DIM_OVERWORLD) {
        int x4,y4,z4;
        voronoiAccess3D(g->sha,x,y,z,&x4,&y4,&z4);
        /* Exact single-point MC_26_50 path. NULL hint preserves tie handling. */
        return sampleBiomeNoise(&g->bn,NULL,x4,y4,z4,NULL,0);
    }
    if (dimension==DIM_END) {
        Range r={1,x,z,1,1,y,1},s=getVoronoiSrcRange(r);
        int64_t a=llabs(floordiv(s.x,4)),b=llabs(floordiv(s.x+s.sx-1,4));
        int64_t c=llabs(floordiv(s.z,4)),d=llabs(floordiv(s.z+s.sz-1,4));
        int64_t mx=a>b?a:b,mz=c>d?c:d;
        if(mx*mx+mz*mz<=4096)return the_end;
        /* The pinned End adapter uses its own planar Voronoi helper. Do not
         * substitute Overworld's 3D nearest-cell rule. */
    }
    return getBiomeAt(g,1,x,y,z);
}
int ss_grid(SeedContext *ctx, int x0, int z0, int nx, int nz, int step,
            int y, int *biomes, double *climate, double *approx_height) {
    if (nx < 1 || nz < 1 || step < 1) return -1;
#ifdef _OPENMP
    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) num_threads(ctx->threads) if(ctx->threads>1 && nx*nz>=256)
#endif
    for (int z = 0; z < nz; z++) for (int x = 0; x < nx; x++) {
        int bx = x0 + x*step, bz = z0 + z*step;
        int i = z*nx + x;
        int64_t np[6];
        biomes[i] = getBiomeAt(&ctx->overworld,1,bx,y,bz);
        /* Depth at y=0, matching this fork's mapApproxHeight heuristic. */
        sampleBiomeNoise(&ctx->overworld.bn,np,floordiv(bx,4),0,
                         floordiv(bz,4),NULL,SAMPLE_NO_BIOME);
        for (int k=0;k<6;k++) climate[6*i+k] = np[k] / 10000.0;
        approx_height[i] = np[NP_DEPTH] / 76.0;
    }
    return 0;
}
int ss_heights(SeedContext *ctx, const int *xz, int n, int vertical_step,
               int *heights, int *surface_biomes) {
    if (n < 0 || vertical_step < 1 || vertical_step > 16) return -1;
    if (!ctx->terrain_ready) {
        initTerrainNoise(&ctx->terrain,ctx->seed);
        initTerrainShaper(&ctx->shaper);
        ctx->terrain_ready = 1;
    }
#ifdef _OPENMP
    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) num_threads(ctx->threads) if(ctx->threads>1 && n>=256)
#endif
    for (int i=0;i<n;i++) {
        int x=xz[2*i], z=xz[2*i+1], top=-65;
        double offset, factor;
        DensityCell cell={INT_MIN,0};
        sampleTerrainShape(&ctx->overworld.bn,&ctx->shaper,
                           floordiv(x,4),floordiv(z,4),&offset,&factor);
        /* Raw density estimate: excludes cave, aquifer, decoration and
           actual chunk interpolation. Coarse scans can miss thin overhangs. */
        for (int y=319;;y-=vertical_step) {
            if (y < -64) y=-64;
            if (density_cell(&ctx->terrain,offset,factor,x,y,z,&cell)>0) {
                top=y;
                for (int yy=y+vertical_step-1;yy>y;yy--) {
                    if (yy<=319 && density_cell(&ctx->terrain,offset,factor,x,yy,z,&cell)>0) {
                        top=yy; break;
                    }
                }
                break;
            }
            if (y==-64) break;
        }
        heights[i]=top;
        int sample_y=top<63 ? 63 : top+1;
        surface_biomes[i]=getBiomeAt(&ctx->overworld,1,x,sample_y,z);
    }
    return 0;
}
static int inside(Pos p,int x0,int z0,int x1,int z1) {
    return p.x>=x0 && p.x<=x1 && p.z>=z0 && p.z<=z1;
}
static int emit(Pos p,int *out,int n,int cap) {
    if (n>=cap) return -2;
    out[2*n]=p.x; out[2*n+1]=p.z;
    return n+1;
}
/* Generator-free adapter, restricted to reviewed low32 MT families. */
int ss_raw_layout(uint32_t low32,int type,int x0,int z0,int x1,int z1,int *xz,int capacity) {
    if (!(type==Village || type==Ancient_City || type==Ruined_Portal ||
          type==Shipwreck || type==Monument || type==Outpost ||
          type==Fortress || type==Bastion)) return -1;
    StructureConfig cfg;
    if (x0>x1 || z0>z1 || capacity<1 || !getStructureConfig(type,MC_26_50,&cfg)) return -1;
    int n=0,size=cfg.regionSize*16;
    for (int rx=floordiv(x0,size)-1;rx<=floordiv(x1,size)+1;rx++)
        for (int rz=floordiv(z0,size)-1;rz<=floordiv(z1,size)+1;rz++) {
            Pos p;
            if (!getStructurePos(type,MC_26_50,(uint64_t)low32,rx,rz,&p) || !inside(p,x0,z0,x1,z1)) continue;
            n=emit(p,xz,n,capacity);
            if (n<0) return n;
        }
    return n;
}
/* Version 1: finite all-input counts, explicit inclusive regions, no generator or
 * viability. Native bounded batches never truncate scan coverage/top selection.
 * getStructurePos re-seeds the pinned thread-local MT for each region. */
int ss_sweep_raw(uint64_t start,uint64_t count,const int *types,const int *bounds,
                 int families,int threads,int *counts,uint64_t capacity) {
    const uint64_t limit=UINT64_C(1)<<32;
    if(start>limit || count>65536 || count>limit-start || families<1 || families>8 ||
       capacity<count*(uint64_t)families || threads<1 || threads>36 ||
       (threads>1 && !ss_openmp_enabled()))return -1;
    StructureConfig configs[8];
    for(int j=0;j<families;j++) {
        int t=types[j];const int *b=bounds+4*j;
        if(!(t==Village || t==Ancient_City || t==Ruined_Portal || t==Shipwreck ||
             t==Monument || t==Outpost || t==Fortress || t==Bastion) ||
           b[0]>b[2] || b[1]>b[3] || !getStructureConfig(t,MC_26_50,&configs[j]))return -1;
        int size=configs[j].regionSize*16;
        /* Bound work per seed, including the exact one-region reference halo. */
        int64_t nx=(int64_t)floordiv(b[2],size)-floordiv(b[0],size)+3;
        int64_t nz=(int64_t)floordiv(b[3],size)-floordiv(b[1],size)+3;
        if(nx<1 || nz<1 || nx>4096 || nz>4096 || nx*nz>4096)return -1;
    }
#ifdef _OPENMP
    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) num_threads(threads) if(threads>1 && count>=64)
#endif
    for(uint64_t i=0;i<count;i++) {
        uint32_t seed=(uint32_t)(start+i);
        for(int j=0;j<families;j++) {
            int size=configs[j].regionSize*16,n=0;const int *b=bounds+4*j;
            for(int rx=floordiv(b[0],size)-1;rx<=floordiv(b[2],size)+1;rx++)
                for(int rz=floordiv(b[1],size)-1;rz<=floordiv(b[3],size)+1;rz++) {
                    Pos p;
                    if(getStructurePos(types[j],MC_26_50,seed,rx,rz,&p) && inside(p,b[0],b[1],b[2],b[3]))n++;
                }
            counts[i*families+j]=n;
        }
    }
    return 0;
}
int ss_biome_points(SeedContext *ctx,int dimension,const int *xyz,int n,int *biomes) {
    if (n<0 || !ss_generator(ctx,dimension) || (n && (!xyz || !biomes))) return -1;
    /* End point queries otherwise build overlapping 27-cell height halos for
     * every point. Share one bounded reference map for dense batches. The
     * exact End planar Voronoi and negative-coordinate rules remain.
     * All temporary memory belongs to this call, before any parallel loop. */
    if (dimension==DIM_END && n>=256 && n<=100000) {
        int *cells=malloc((size_t)n*4*sizeof(*cells));
        if (cells) {
            int x0=INT_MAX,z0=INT_MAX,x1=INT_MIN,z1=INT_MIN;
            for (int i=0;i<n;i++) {
                Range r={1,xyz[3*i],xyz[3*i+2],1,1,xyz[3*i+1],1},s=getVoronoiSrcRange(r);
                cells[4*i]=s.x;cells[4*i+1]=s.z;cells[4*i+2]=s.sx;cells[4*i+3]=s.sz;
                int cx=floordiv(s.x,4),cz=floordiv(s.z,4);
                int hx=floordiv(s.x+s.sx-1,4),hz=floordiv(s.z+s.sz-1,4);
                if(cx<x0)x0=cx;if(hx>x1)x1=hx;
                if(cz<z0)z0=cz;if(hz>z1)z1=hz;
            }
            int64_t w=(int64_t)x1-x0+1,h=(int64_t)z1-z0+1,area=w*h;
            if(w<=1024 && h<=1024 && area<=65536 && area<=(int64_t)n*128) {
                int *map=malloc((size_t)area*sizeof(*map));
                if(map) {
                    mapEndBiome(&ctx->end.en,map,x0,z0,(int)w,(int)h);
                    for(int i=0;i<n;i++) {
                        int px=cells[4*i],pz=cells[4*i+1],pw=cells[4*i+2],ph=cells[4*i+3],src[9];
                        for(int j=0;j<ph;j++)for(int k=0;k<pw;k++)
                            src[j*pw+k]=map[(floordiv(pz+j,4)-z0)*w+floordiv(px+k,4)-x0];
                        mapVoronoiPlane(ctx->end.sha,biomes+i,src,xyz[3*i],xyz[3*i+2],1,1,xyz[3*i+1],px,pz,pw,ph);
                    }
                    free(map);free(cells);return 0;
                }
            }
            free(cells);
        }
    }
#ifdef _OPENMP
    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) num_threads(ctx->threads) if(ctx->threads>1 && n>=256)
#endif
    for (int i=0;i<n;i++) biomes[i]=ss_biome(ctx,dimension,xyz[3*i],xyz[3*i+1],xyz[3*i+2]);
    return 0;
}
#include "filters.inc"
int ss_scan(SeedContext *ctx,int type,int x0,int z0,int x1,int z1,
            int viable_only,int *xz,int capacity) {
    StructureConfig cfg;
    if (x0>x1 || z0>z1 || capacity<1 || !getStructureConfig(type,MC_26_50,&cfg)) return -1;
    int n=0;
    if (type==Mineshaft) {
return ss_mineshaft_inventory(ctx,x0,z0,x1,z1,xz,capacity);
    }
    int size=cfg.regionSize*16;
    Generator *g=ss_generator(ctx,cfg.dim);
    if (!g) return -1;
    /* Only Stronghold has reviewed starts displaced outside its region. */
    int halo=type==Stronghold ? 1 : 0;
    for (int rx=floordiv(x0,size)-halo;rx<=floordiv(x1,size)+halo;rx++) {
        for (int rz=floordiv(z0,size)-halo;rz<=floordiv(z1,size)+halo;rz++) {
            Pos p;
            int found=getStructurePos(type,MC_26_50,ctx->seed,rx,rz,&p);
            /* Keep the selected Bedrock structure type. Promoting a rejected
             * Bastion to a Fortress produced the absent seed-12345 start at
             * (-152,120); phone inspection supplied the negative fixture. */
            if (!found || !inside(p,x0,z0,x1,z1)) continue;
            if (viable_only && type!=Stronghold && !isViableStructurePos(type,g,p.x,p.z,0)) continue;
            n=emit(p,xz,n,capacity);
            if (n<0) return n;
        }
    }
    if (type==Stronghold) {
        StrongholdIter it={0};
        for (int i=0;i<3;i++) {
            nextVillageStronghold(&it,&ctx->overworld);
            if (inside(it.pos,x0,z0,x1,z1)) {
                n=emit(it.pos,xz,n,capacity);
                if (n<0) return n;
            }
        }
    }
    return n;
}
int ss_attempt(SeedContext *ctx,int type,int rx,int rz,int *xz) {
    if (type<0 || type==Mineshaft) return -1;
    Pos p;
    int ok=getStructurePos(type,MC_26_50,ctx->seed,rx,rz,&p);
    if (ok) { xz[0]=p.x; xz[1]=p.z; }
    return ok;
}
/* Validate enumerated starts without repeating placement or Stronghold iteration.
 * Match ss_scan's special adapters: those two families never veto raw starts. */
int ss_filter_starts(SeedContext *ctx,int type,const int *xz,int n,int *viable) {
    StructureConfig cfg;
    if (n<0 || !getStructureConfig(type,MC_26_50,&cfg)) return -1;
    Generator *g=ss_generator(ctx,cfg.dim);
    if (!g) return -1;
    for (int i=0;i<n;i++)
        viable[i]=(type==Stronghold || type==Mineshaft) ? 1 :
            !!isViableStructurePos(type,g,xz[2*i],xz[2*i+1],0);
    return 0;
}
/* Expose the actual viability sampling convention for evidence, not a new rule.
 * out: quart x/y/z, sampled biome, rotation, variant, offset x/z, size x/z. */
int ss_diagnostic(SeedContext *ctx, int type, int x, int z, int *out) {
    if (type == Ancient_City) {
        out[0]=x>>2; out[1]=-27>>2; out[2]=z>>2;
        out[3]=getBiomeAt(&ctx->overworld,0,out[0],out[1],out[2]);
        for (int i=4;i<10;i++) out[i]=0;
        return 1;
    }
    if (type != Bastion) return 0;
    StructureVariant sv;
    if (!getVariant(&sv,Bastion,MC_26_50,ctx->seed,x,z,-1)) return 0;
    int cx=x>>4,cz=z>>4;
    out[0]=(cx*32+2*sv.x+sv.sx-1)/2>>2;
    out[1]=33>>2;
    out[2]=(cz*32+2*sv.z+sv.sz-1)/2>>2;
    out[3]=getBiomeAt(&ctx->nether,4,out[0],out[1],out[2]);
    out[4]=sv.rotation;out[5]=sv.start;
    out[6]=sv.x;out[7]=sv.z;out[8]=sv.sx;out[9]=sv.sz;
    return 1;
}
/* Exact sea-level classification relative to the stride-one raw density model.
 * Checking upward permits early success for land while retaining thin overhangs.
 * Unlike a monotonic/binary-search shortcut, every Y>=63 is checked for water. */
static int land_column(SeedContext *ctx,int x,int z) {
    double offset,factor;
    DensityCell cell={INT_MIN,0};
    sampleTerrainShape(&ctx->overworld.bn,&ctx->shaper,floordiv(x,4),floordiv(z,4),&offset,&factor);
    for(int y=63;y<=319;y++)
        if(density_cell(&ctx->terrain,offset,factor,x,y,z,&cell)>0)return 1;
    return 0;
}
typedef struct LandCell { int x,z,representative; } LandCell;
static uint64_t land_hash(int x,int z) {
    uint64_t key=((uint64_t)(uint32_t)x<<32)|(uint32_t)z;
    key^=key>>30;key*=UINT64_C(0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9);
    key^=key>>27;key*=UINT64_C(0x94d049bb133111eb);
    return key^(key>>31);
}
int ss_land(SeedContext *ctx, const int *xz, int n, int *land) {
    if (n<0 || n>100000) return -1;
    if (!ctx->terrain_ready) {
        initTerrainNoise(&ctx->terrain,ctx->seed);
        initTerrainShaper(&ctx->shaper);
        ctx->terrain_ready=1;
    }
    if(n<16) {
        for(int i=0;i<n;i++)land[i]=land_column(ctx,xz[2*i],xz[2*i+1]);
        return 0;
    }
    size_t size=16;
    while(size<(size_t)n*2)size*=2;
    LandCell *cells=calloc(size,sizeof(*cells));
    int *representatives=malloc((size_t)n*sizeof(*representatives));
    if(!cells || !representatives) {free(cells);free(representatives);return -1;}
    for(int i=0;i<n;i++) {
        int x=floordiv(xz[2*i],4),z=floordiv(xz[2*i+1],4);
        size_t slot=(size_t)land_hash(x,z)&(size-1);
        while(cells[slot].representative && (cells[slot].x!=x || cells[slot].z!=z))slot=(slot+1)&(size-1);
        if(!cells[slot].representative) {
            cells[slot]=(LandCell){x,z,i+1};
        }
        representatives[i]=cells[slot].representative-1;
    }
    /* Immutable mapping is constructed serially. Each representative/output has
     * one owner; broadcast only after the team joins. Sampling is read-only. */
#ifdef _OPENMP
    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) num_threads(ctx->threads) if(ctx->threads>1 && n>=256)
#endif
    for(int i=0;i<n;i++)if(representatives[i]==i)land[i]=land_column(ctx,xz[2*i],xz[2*i+1]);
    for(int i=0;i<n;i++)land[i]=land[representatives[i]];
    free(cells);free(representatives);
    return 0;
}

/* Diagnostic interface for exact raw-density parity/calibration. */
int ss_density_points(SeedContext *ctx,const int *xyz,int n,double *out) {
    if(n<0 || n>100000)return -1;
    if(!ctx->terrain_ready) {
        initTerrainNoise(&ctx->terrain,ctx->seed);
        initTerrainShaper(&ctx->shaper);ctx->terrain_ready=1;
    }
    for(int i=0;i<n;i++) {
        int x=xyz[3*i],y=xyz[3*i+1],z=xyz[3*i+2];double offset,factor;
        DensityCell cell={INT_MIN,0};
        sampleTerrainShape(&ctx->overworld.bn,&ctx->shaper,floordiv(x,4),floordiv(z,4),&offset,&factor);
        out[i]=density_cell(&ctx->terrain,offset,factor,x,y,z,&cell);
    }
    return 0;
}


========================================================================
FILE: native/filters.inc
========================================================================
/* Exact pinned-predictor filters. Included after bridge helpers; no shared MT
 * state is used by the four-lane Mineshaft inventory kernel. */
static uint32_t ss_temper(uint32_t y) {
    y^=y>>11; y^=(y<<7)&UINT32_C(0x9d2c5680);
    y^=(y<<15)&UINT32_C(0xefc60000); return y^(y>>18);
}
static uint32_t ss_twist(uint32_t a,uint32_t b) {
    return (((a&UINT32_C(0x80000000))|(b&UINT32_C(0x7fffffff)))>>1)
        ^((b&1)?UINT32_C(0x9908b0df):0);
}
static void ss_first3(uint32_t seed,uint32_t *out) {
    uint32_t early[4]={seed,0,0,0},v=seed;
    for(uint32_t i=1;i<=399;i++) {
        v=UINT32_C(1812433253)*(v^(v>>30))+i;
        if(i<4)early[i]=v;
        if(i>=397)out[i-397]=ss_temper(v^ss_twist(early[i-397],early[i-396]));
    }
}
#if defined(__GNUC__) || defined(__clang__)
typedef uint32_t SSU4 __attribute__((vector_size(16)));
static SSU4 ss_temper4(SSU4 y) {
    y^=y>>11; y^=(y<<7)&UINT32_C(0x9d2c5680);
    y^=(y<<15)&UINT32_C(0xefc60000); return y^(y>>18);
}
static void ss_second_third4(SSU4 seed,SSU4 *second,SSU4 *third) {
    SSU4 u1=UINT32_C(1812433253)*(seed^(seed>>30))+1;
    SSU4 u2=UINT32_C(1812433253)*(u1^(u1>>30))+2;
    SSU4 u3=UINT32_C(1812433253)*(u2^(u2>>30))+3,v=u3;
    for(uint32_t i=4;i<=398;i++)v=UINT32_C(1812433253)*(v^(v>>30))+i;
    SSU4 a=(((u1&UINT32_C(0x80000000))|(u2&UINT32_C(0x7fffffff)))>>1)^((u2&1)*UINT32_C(0x9908b0df));
    *second=ss_temper4(v^a);
    v=UINT32_C(1812433253)*(v^(v>>30))+399;
    a=(((u2&UINT32_C(0x80000000))|(u3&UINT32_C(0x7fffffff)))>>1)^((u3&1)*UINT32_C(0x9908b0df));
    *third=ss_temper4(v^a);
}
#endif
static int ss_mineshaft_query(SeedContext *ctx,int x0,int z0,int x1,int z1,int *xz,int cap,int first) {
    if(!ctx || !xz || cap<1 || x0>x1 || z0>z1 ||
       x0<-(1<<30) || z0<-(1<<30) || x1>=(1<<30) || z1>=(1<<30) ||
       (int64_t)x1-x0>14000 || (int64_t)z1-z0>14000)return -1;
    int cx0=-floordiv(8-x0,16),cz0=-floordiv(8-z0,16);
    int cx1=floordiv(x1-8,16),cz1=floordiv(z1-8,16),n=0;
    if(cx0>cx1 || cz0>cz1)return 0;
#if defined(__GNUC__) || defined(__clang__)
    uint32_t world[3];ss_first3((uint32_t)ctx->seed,world);
    uint32_t a=world[0]>>1,b=world[1]>>1;
    for(int x=cx0;x<=cx1;x++)for(int z=cz0;z<=cz1;z+=4) {
        SSU4 seeds;
        for(int lane=0;lane<4;lane++)seeds[lane]=((uint32_t)x*a)^((uint32_t)(z+lane)*b)^(uint32_t)ctx->seed;
        SSU4 second,third;ss_second_third4(seeds,&second,&third);
        for(int lane=0;lane<4 && z+lane<=cz1;lane++) {
            int zz=z+lane;
            if((float)(second[lane]*(1.0/4294967296.0))<0.004 &&
               third[lane]%80<(uint32_t)(abs(x)>abs(zz)?abs(x):abs(zz))) {
                n=emit((Pos){x*16+8,zz*16+8},xz,n,cap);
                if(n<0 || first)return n;
            }
        }
    }
#else
    for(int x=cx0;x<=cx1;x++)for(int z=cz0;z<=cz1;z++) {
        Pos p;
        if(getMineshafts(MC_26_50,ctx->seed,x,z,x,z,&p,1)) {
            n=emit(p,xz,n,cap);if(n<0 || first)return n;
        }
    }
#endif
    return n;
}
int ss_mineshaft_first(SeedContext *ctx,int x0,int z0,int x1,int z1,int *xz) {
    return ss_mineshaft_query(ctx,x0,z0,x1,z1,xz,1,1);
}
int ss_mineshaft_inventory(SeedContext *ctx,int x0,int z0,int x1,int z1,int *xz,int cap) {
    return ss_mineshaft_query(ctx,x0,z0,x1,z1,xz,cap,0);
}
int ss_structure_first(SeedContext *ctx,int type,int x0,int z0,int x1,int z1,int *xz) {
    if(type==Mineshaft)return ss_mineshaft_first(ctx,x0,z0,x1,z1,xz);
    StructureConfig cfg;
    if(!ctx || !xz || x0>x1 || z0>z1 || type==Stronghold || !getStructureConfig(type,MC_26_50,&cfg) ||
       x0<-(1<<30) || z0<-(1<<30) || x1>=(1<<30) || z1>=(1<<30) ||
       (int64_t)x1-x0>14000 || (int64_t)z1-z0>14000)return -1;
    Generator *g=ss_generator(ctx,cfg.dim);if(!g)return -1;
    int size=cfg.regionSize*16;
    for(int rx=floordiv(x0,size);rx<=floordiv(x1,size);rx++)
        for(int rz=floordiv(z0,size);rz<=floordiv(z1,size);rz++) {
            Pos p;
            if(getStructurePos(type,MC_26_50,ctx->seed,rx,rz,&p) && inside(p,x0,z0,x1,z1) &&
               isViableStructurePos(type,g,p.x,p.z,0)) {
                xz[0]=p.x;xz[1]=p.z;return 1;
            }
        }
    return 0;
}
int ss_nether_pair(SeedContext *ctx,int x0,int z0,int x1,int z1,int *xzt,int *viable,int cap) {
    StructureConfig cfg;
    if(!ctx || !xzt || !viable || x0>x1 || z0>z1 || cap<1 || !getStructureConfig(Fortress,MC_26_50,&cfg) ||
       x0<-(1<<30) || z0<-(1<<30) || x1>=(1<<30) || z1>=(1<<30) ||
       (int64_t)x1-x0>14000 || (int64_t)z1-z0>14000)return -1;
    int n=0,size=cfg.regionSize*16;
    for(int rx=floordiv(x0,size);rx<=floordiv(x1,size);rx++)
        for(int rz=floordiv(z0,size);rz<=floordiv(z1,size);rz++) {
            Pos p;int fortress=getStructurePos(Fortress,MC_26_50,ctx->seed,rx,rz,&p);
            if(!inside(p,x0,z0,x1,z1))continue;
            if(n>=cap)return -2;
            int type=fortress?Fortress:Bastion;
            xzt[3*n]=p.x;xzt[3*n+1]=p.z;xzt[3*n+2]=fortress;
            viable[n]=!!isViableStructurePos(type,&ctx->nether,p.x,p.z,0);n++;
        }
    return n;
}
static int ss_guaranteed(int type,const int *b) {
    if(type!=Village && type!=Ancient_City)return 0;
    StructureConfig cfg;if(!getStructureConfig(type,MC_26_50,&cfg))return 0;
    int size=cfg.regionSize*16,last=(cfg.chunkRange-1)*16+8;
    int rx0=-floordiv(8-b[0],size),rz0=-floordiv(8-b[1],size);
    int rx1=floordiv(b[2]-last,size),rz1=floordiv(b[3]-last,size);
    if(rx0>rx1 || rz0>rz1)return 0;
    return (rx1-rx0+1)*(rz1-rz0+1);
}
static int ss_capped_raw(uint32_t seed,int type,const int *b,int cap) {
    StructureConfig cfg;getStructureConfig(type,MC_26_50,&cfg);
    if(ss_guaranteed(type,b)>=cap)return cap;
    int n=0,size=cfg.regionSize*16;
    for(int rx=floordiv(b[0],size);rx<=floordiv(b[2],size);rx++)
        for(int rz=floordiv(b[1],size);rz<=floordiv(b[3],size);rz++) {
            Pos p;if(getStructurePos(type,MC_26_50,seed,rx,rz,&p) && inside(p,b[0],b[1],b[2],b[3])) {
                if(++n>=cap)return n;
            }
        }
    return n;
}
/* mode 0=reference full, 1=presence, 2=capped at 32, 3=trim/shared full.
 * Only the reviewed five-family predicate is accepted by optimized modes. */
int ss_sweep_raw_mode(uint64_t start,uint64_t count,const int *types,const int *bounds,
                      int families,int threads,int *counts,uint64_t capacity,int mode) {
    if(mode==0)return ss_sweep_raw(start,count,types,bounds,families,threads,counts,capacity);
    const uint64_t limit=UINT64_C(1)<<32;
    if(mode<1 || mode>3 || start>limit || count>65536 || count>limit-start ||
       families!=5 || !types || !bounds || !counts || capacity<count*5 || threads<1 || threads>36 ||
       (threads>1 && !ss_openmp_enabled()))return -1;
    if(types[0]!=Village || types[1]!=Ancient_City || types[2]!=Fortress || types[3]!=Bastion || types[4]!=Fortress)return -1;
    const int *small=bounds+8,*large=bounds+16;
    for(int k=0;k<4;k++)if(small[k]!=bounds[12+k])return -1;
    if(small[0]<large[0] || small[1]<large[1] || small[2]>large[2] || small[3]>large[3])return -1;
    StructureConfig fc;getStructureConfig(Fortress,MC_26_50,&fc);int size=fc.regionSize*16;
    for(int j=0;j<5;j++) {
        StructureConfig cfg;getStructureConfig(types[j],MC_26_50,&cfg);
        const int *b=bounds+4*j;
        int64_t nx=(int64_t)floordiv(b[2],cfg.regionSize*16)-floordiv(b[0],cfg.regionSize*16)+1;
        int64_t nz=(int64_t)floordiv(b[3],cfg.regionSize*16)-floordiv(b[1],cfg.regionSize*16)+1;
        if(b[0]>b[2] || b[1]>b[3] || nx<1 || nz<1 || nx*nz>4096)return -1;
    }
#ifdef _OPENMP
    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) num_threads(threads) if(threads>1 && count>=64)
#endif
    for(uint64_t i=0;i<count;i++) {
        uint32_t seed=(uint32_t)(start+i);int *out=counts+i*5;
        int cap=mode==1?1:mode==2?32:INT_MAX;
        out[0]=ss_capped_raw(seed,Village,bounds,cap);out[1]=ss_capped_raw(seed,Ancient_City,bounds+4,cap);
        out[2]=out[3]=out[4]=0;
        /* Presence scans the small envelope first; shared final Fortress implies
         * initial presence, avoiding an unnecessary broad-region scan. */
        const int *scan=mode==1?small:large;
        for(int rx=floordiv(scan[0],size);rx<=floordiv(scan[2],size);rx++) {
            for(int rz=floordiv(scan[1],size);rz<=floordiv(scan[3],size);rz++) {
                Pos p;int fortress=getStructurePos(Fortress,MC_26_50,seed,rx,rz,&p);
                if(fortress && inside(p,large[0],large[1],large[2],large[3]) && out[4]<cap)out[4]++;
                if(inside(p,small[0],small[1],small[2],small[3])) {
                    int slot=fortress?2:3;if(out[slot]<cap)out[slot]++;
                }
                if(out[2]>=cap && out[3]>=cap && out[4]>=cap)break;
            }
            if(out[2]>=cap && out[3]>=cap && out[4]>=cap)break;
        }
        if(mode==1 && !out[4])out[4]=ss_capped_raw(seed,Fortress,large,1);
    }
    return 0;
}


========================================================================
FILE: vendor/cubiomes-bedrock/finders.c
========================================================================
#include "finders.h"
#include "biomes.h"
#include "util.h"

#include <stdio.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <limits.h>
#include <float.h>
#include <math.h>


#define PI 3.14159265358979323846


//==============================================================================
// Finding Structure Positions
//==============================================================================


int getStructureConfig(int structureType, int mc, StructureConfig *sconf)
{
    static const StructureConfig
    s_feature               = { 14357617, 32, 24, Feature,          DIM_OVERWORLD, 0,      BEDROCK_RANDOM},
    s_desert_pyramid        = { 14357617, 32, 24, Desert_Pyramid,   DIM_OVERWORLD, 0,      BEDROCK_RANDOM},
    s_igloo                 = { 14357617, 32, 24, Igloo,            DIM_OVERWORLD, 0,      BEDROCK_RANDOM},
    s_jungle_temple         = { 14357617, 32, 24, Jungle_Pyramid,   DIM_OVERWORLD, 0,      BEDROCK_RANDOM},
    s_swamp_hut             = { 14357617, 32, 24, Swamp_Hut,        DIM_OVERWORLD, 0,      BEDROCK_RANDOM},
    s_outpost               = {165745296, 80, 56, Outpost,          DIM_OVERWORLD, 0,      BEDROCK_RANDOM},
    s_village_110           = { 10387312,  1,  1, Village,          DIM_OVERWORLD, 50,     BEDROCK_RANDOM},
    s_village_117           = { 10387312, 27, 17, Village,          DIM_OVERWORLD, 0.2,    BEDROCK_RANDOM},
    s_village_1730          = { 10387312, 27, 17, Village,          DIM_OVERWORLD, 0.02,   BEDROCK_RANDOM},
    s_village               = { 10387312, 34, 26, Village,          DIM_OVERWORLD, 0.02,   BEDROCK_RANDOM},
    s_stronghold            = { 97858791,200,150, Stronghold,       DIM_OVERWORLD, 0.25,   BEDROCK_RANDOM},
    s_ocean_ruin_117        = { 14357621, 12,  5, Ocean_Ruin,       DIM_OVERWORLD, 0,      BEDROCK_RANDOM},
    s_ocean_ruin            = { 14357621, 20, 12, Ocean_Ruin,       DIM_OVERWORLD, 0,      BEDROCK_RANDOM},
    s_shipwreck_117         = {165745295, 10,  5, Shipwreck,        DIM_OVERWORLD, 0,      BEDROCK_RANDOM},
    s_shipwreck             = {165745295, 24, 20, Shipwreck,        DIM_OVERWORLD, 0,      BEDROCK_RANDOM},
    s_monument              = { 10387313, 32, 27, Monument,         DIM_OVERWORLD, 0,      BEDROCK_RANDOM},
    s_mansion               = { 10387319, 80, 60, Mansion,          DIM_OVERWORLD, 0,      BEDROCK_RANDOM},
    s_ruined_portal         = { 40552231, 40, 25, Ruined_Portal,    DIM_OVERWORLD, 0,      BEDROCK_RANDOM},
    s_ancient_city          = { 20083232, 24, 16, Ancient_City,     DIM_OVERWORLD, 0,      BEDROCK_RANDOM},
    s_trail_ruins           = { 83469867, 34, 26, Trail_Ruins,      DIM_OVERWORLD, 0,         JAVA_RANDOM},
    s_trial_chambers        = { 94251327, 34, 22, Trial_Chambers,   DIM_OVERWORLD, 0,         JAVA_RANDOM},
    s_abandoned_camp_264    = { 91231127, 34, 26, Abandoned_Camp,   DIM_OVERWORLD, 0,      BEDROCK_RANDOM},
    s_abandoned_camp        = { 91231127, 37, 29, Abandoned_Camp,   DIM_OVERWORLD, 0,         JAVA_RANDOM},
    s_treasure              = { 16842397,  4,  2, Treasure,         DIM_OVERWORLD, 0,      BEDROCK_RANDOM},
    s_mineshaft             = {        0,  1,  1, Mineshaft,        DIM_OVERWORLD, 0,      BEDROCK_RANDOM},
    // nether structures
    s_ruined_portal_n       = { 40552231, 25, 15, Ruined_Portal_N,  DIM_NETHER, 0,         BEDROCK_RANDOM},
    s_fortress_114          = {        0, 16,  8, Fortress,         DIM_NETHER, 0,         BEDROCK_RANDOM},
    s_fortress              = { 30084232, 30, 26, Fortress,         DIM_NETHER, 0,         BEDROCK_RANDOM},
    s_bastion               = { 30084232, 30, 26, Bastion,          DIM_NETHER, 0,         BEDROCK_RANDOM},
    // end structures
    s_end_city              = { 10387313, 20,  9, End_City,         DIM_END,   0,          BEDROCK_RANDOM},
    s_end_gateway           = {        0,  1,  1, End_Gateway,      DIM_END, 700,          BEDROCK_RANDOM},
    // decorators
    s_desert_well           = {-1160484816,  1,  1, Desert_Well,    DIM_OVERWORLD, 500,    BEDROCK_RANDOM},
    s_geode_117             = { 1974035328,  1,  1, Geode,          DIM_OVERWORLD,  53,    BEDROCK_RANDOM},
    s_geode                 = { 1974035328,  1,  1, Geode,          DIM_OVERWORLD,  24,    BEDROCK_RANDOM},
    s_dungeon               = {          0,  1,  1, Dungeon,        DIM_OVERWORLD,   0,    BEDROCK_RANDOM},
    s_end_island            = {          0,  1,  1, End_Island,     DIM_END,        14,    BEDROCK_RANDOM},
    s_ravine_215            = {          0,  1,  1, Ravine,         DIM_OVERWORLD, 150,    BEDROCK_RANDOM},
    s_ravine                = {          0,  1,  1, Ravine,         DIM_OVERWORLD, 100,    BEDROCK_RANDOM},
    s_lavalake              = {          0,  1,  1, Lava_Lake,      DIM_OVERWORLD,   8,    BEDROCK_RANDOM}
    ;

    switch (structureType)
    {
    case Feature:
        *sconf = s_feature;
        return mc <= MC_UNDEF;
    case Desert_Pyramid:
        *sconf = s_desert_pyramid;
        return mc >= MC_UNDEF;// 0.13.0
    case Jungle_Pyramid:
        *sconf = s_jungle_temple;
        return mc >= MC_UNDEF;// 0.15.0
    case Swamp_Hut:
        *sconf = s_swamp_hut;
        return mc >= MC_UNDEF;// 0.14.0
    case Igloo:
        *sconf = s_igloo;
        return mc >= MC_1_0;
    case Village:
        if (mc >= MC_1_18)
            *sconf = s_village;
        else if (mc >= MC_1_17_30)
            *sconf = s_village_1730;
        else if (mc >= MC_1_11)
            *sconf = s_village_117;
        else
            *sconf = s_village_110;
        return mc >= MC_UNDEF;// 0.9.0
    case Stronghold:
        *sconf = s_stronghold;
        return mc >= MC_UNDEF;// 0.9.0
    case Ocean_Ruin:
        *sconf = mc < MC_1_18 ? s_ocean_ruin_117 : s_ocean_ruin;
        return mc >= MC_1_4;
    case Shipwreck:
        *sconf = mc < MC_1_18 ? s_shipwreck_117 : s_shipwreck;
        return mc >= MC_1_4;
    case Ruined_Portal:
        *sconf = s_ruined_portal;
        return mc >= MC_1_16;
    case Ruined_Portal_N:
        *sconf = s_ruined_portal_n;
        return mc >= MC_1_16;
    case Monument:
        *sconf = s_monument;
        return mc >= MC_UNDEF;// 0.16.0
    case End_City:
        *sconf = s_end_city;
        return mc >= MC_1_0; 
    case Mansion:
        *sconf = s_mansion;
        return mc >= MC_1_1;
    case Outpost:
        *sconf = s_outpost;
        return mc >= MC_1_11;
    case Ancient_City:
        *sconf = s_ancient_city;
        return mc >= MC_1_19;
    case Treasure:
        *sconf = s_treasure;
        return mc >= MC_UNDEF;// 0.9.0
    case Mineshaft:
        *sconf = s_mineshaft;
        return mc >= MC_UNDEF;// 0.9.0
    case Ravine:
        *sconf = mc < MC_1_21_60 ? s_ravine_215 : s_ravine;
        return mc >= MC_1_2;
    case Fortress:
        *sconf = mc <= MC_1_14 ? s_fortress_114 : s_fortress;
        return mc >= MC_1_0;
    case Bastion:
        *sconf = s_bastion;
        return mc >= MC_1_16;
    case End_Gateway:
        *sconf = s_end_gateway;
        return mc >= MC_1_0; 
    case End_Island:
        *sconf = s_end_island;
        // exists pre-1.6 but logic unknown
        return mc >= MC_1_6;
    case Desert_Well:
        *sconf = s_desert_well;
        // wells were introduced in 1.2, but we only support decorator features
        // for 1.13+
        return mc >= MC_1_13;
    case Lava_Lake:
        *sconf = s_lavalake;
        return mc >= MC_1_0;
    case Geode:
        *sconf = mc < MC_1_18 ? s_geode_117 : s_geode;
        return mc >= MC_1_17;
    case Dungeon:
        *sconf = s_dungeon;
        // only 1.18+ dungeons are supported
        // the cave terrain uses 1.18+
        return mc >= MC_1_18;
    case Trail_Ruins:
        *sconf = s_trail_ruins;
        return mc >= MC_1_20;
    case Trial_Chambers:
        *sconf = s_trial_chambers;
        return mc >= MC_1_21;
    case Abandoned_Camp:
        *sconf = mc >= MC_26_50 ? s_abandoned_camp : s_abandoned_camp_264;
        return mc >= MC_26_40; // 26.40.27
    default:
        memset(sconf, 0, sizeof(StructureConfig));
        return 0;
    }
}



int getStructurePos(int structureType, int mc, uint64_t seed, int regX, int regZ, Pos *pos)
{
    StructureConfig sconf;
#if STRUCT_CONFIG_OVERRIDE
    if (!getStructureConfig_override(structureType, mc, &sconf))
#else
    if (!getStructureConfig(structureType, mc, &sconf))
#endif
    {
        return 0;
    }

    switch (structureType)
    {
    case Feature:
    case Desert_Pyramid:
    case Jungle_Pyramid:
    case Swamp_Hut:
    case Igloo:
    case Ruined_Portal:
    case Ruined_Portal_N:
    case Trail_Ruins:
    case Trial_Chambers:
        *pos = getFeaturePos(sconf, seed, regX, regZ);
        return 1;

    case Ocean_Ruin:
	case Shipwreck:
		*pos = (mc < MC_1_18 ? getLargeStructurePos : getFeaturePos)(sconf, seed, regX, regZ);
		return 1;
