/**
 * Gets the min/max parameter values within which a biome change can occur.
 */
const int *getBiomeParaExtremes(int mc)
{
    if (mc <= MC_B1_7)
    {
        static const int extremes_beta[] = {
            0, 10000,
            0, 10000,
            0,0, 0,0, 0,0, 0,0,
        };
        return extremes_beta;
    }
    if (mc < MC_1_18)
        return NULL;
    static const int extremes[] = {
        -4501, 5500,
        -3500, 6999,
        -10500, 300,
        -7799, 5500,
        1000, 10500, // depth has more dependencies
        -9333, 9333,
    };
    return extremes;
}

/**
 * Gets the min/max possible noise parameter values at which the given biome
 * can generate. The values are in min/max pairs in order:
 * temperature, humidity, continentalness, erosion, depth, weirdness.
 */
const int *getBiomeParaLimits(int mc, int id)
{
    if (mc < MC_1_18)
        return NULL;
    int i;
    if (mc > MC_26_40)
    {
        for (i = 0; g_biome_para_range_266_diff[i][0] != -1; i++)
        {
            if (g_biome_para_range_266_diff[i][0] == id)
                return &g_biome_para_range_266_diff[i][1];
        }
    }
    if (mc > MC_26_30)
    {
        for (i = 0; g_biome_para_range_264_diff[i][0] != -1; i++)
        {
            if (g_biome_para_range_264_diff[i][0] == id)
                return &g_biome_para_range_264_diff[i][1];
        }
    }
    if (mc > MC_1_21_60)
    {
        for (i = 0; g_biome_para_range_262_diff[i][0] != -1; i++)
        {
            if (g_biome_para_range_262_diff[i][0] == id)
                return &g_biome_para_range_262_diff[i][1];
        }
    }
    if (mc > MC_1_21)
    {
        for (i = 0; g_biome_para_range_21wd_diff[i][0] != -1; i++)
        {
            if (g_biome_para_range_21wd_diff[i][0] == id)
                return &g_biome_para_range_21wd_diff[i][1];
        }
    }
    if (mc > MC_1_19)
    {
        for (i = 0; g_biome_para_range_20_diff[i][0] != -1; i++)
        {
            if (g_biome_para_range_20_diff[i][0] == id)
                return &g_biome_para_range_20_diff[i][1];
        }
    }
    if (mc > MC_1_18)
    {
        for (i = 0; g_biome_para_range_19_diff[i][0] != -1; i++)
        {
            if (g_biome_para_range_19_diff[i][0] == id)
                return &g_biome_para_range_19_diff[i][1];
        }
    }
    for (i = 0; g_biome_para_range_18[i][0] != -1; i++)
    {
        if (g_biome_para_range_18[i][0] == id)
            return &g_biome_para_range_18[i][1];
    }
    return NULL;
}

/**
 * Determines which biomes are able to generate given climate parameter limits.
 * Possible biomes are marked non-zero in the 'ids'.
 */
void getPossibleBiomesForLimits(char ids[256], int mc, int limits[6][2])
{
    int i, j;
    memset(ids, 0, 256*sizeof(char));

    for (i = 0; i < 256; i++)
    {
        if (!isOverworld(mc, i))
            continue;
        const int *bp = getBiomeParaLimits(mc, i);
        if (!bp)
            continue;

        for (j = 0; j < 6; j++)
        {
            if (limits[j][0] > bp[2*j+1] || limits[j][1] < bp[2*j+0])
                break;
        }
        if (j >= 6)
            ids[bp[-1]] = 1;
    }
}

int getLargestRec(int match, const int *ids, int sx, int sz, Pos *p0, Pos *p1)
{
    typedef struct { int n, j, w; } entry_t;
    entry_t *meta = (entry_t*) calloc(sx > sz ? sx : sz, sizeof(*meta));
    int i, j, w, m, ret;
    ret = m = 0;

    for (i = sx-1; i >= 0; i--)
    {
        for (j = 0; j < sz; j++)
        {
            if (ids[j*sx + i] == match)
                meta[j].n++;
            else
                meta[j].n = 0;
        }
        for (w = j = 0; j < sz; j++)
        {
            int n = meta[j].n;
            if (n > w)
            {
                meta[m].j = j;
                meta[m].w = w;
                m++;
                w = n;
            }
            if (n == w)
                continue;
            do
            {
                entry_t e = meta[--m];
                int area = w * (j - e.j);
                if (area > ret)
                {
                    p0->x = i; p0->z = e.j;
                    p1->x = i+w-1; p1->z = j-1;
                    ret = area;
                }
                w = e.w;
            }
            while (n < w);
            if ((w = n))
                m++;
        }
    }
    free(meta);
    return ret;
}




========================================================================
FILE: vendor/cubiomes-bedrock/mt.c
========================================================================
/*
   A C-program for MT19937, with initialization improved 2002/1/26.
   Coded by Takuji Nishimura and Makoto Matsumoto.
   
   Copyright (C) 1997 - 2002, Makoto Matsumoto and Takuji Nishimura,
   All rights reserved.
   
   http://www.math.sci.hiroshima-u.ac.jp/~m-mat/MT/emt.html
*/

#include <stdio.h>
#include "mt.h"

#define SIZE 624
#define PERIOD 397
#define DIFF (SIZE - PERIOD)

static const uint32_t MAGIC = 0x9908b0df;

struct MTState_
{
  uint32_t MT[SIZE];
  size_t index;
  size_t index_fast; /* lazy init progress */
};

typedef struct MTState_ MTState;

#ifdef _MSC_VER
__declspec(thread) static MTState state;
__declspec(thread) uint64_t mt_ws = (uint64_t)-1;
__declspec(thread) int mt_a;
__declspec(thread) int mt_b;
__declspec(thread) int mt_initialized;
#else
static __thread MTState state;
__thread uint64_t mt_ws = (uint64_t)-1;
__thread int mt_a;
__thread int mt_b;
__thread int mt_initialized;
#endif

#define M32(x) ((x) & 0x80000000)
#define L31(x) ((x) & 0x7FFFFFFF)
#define MIXBITS(u,v) (M32(u) | L31(v))
#define TWIST(u,v) ((MIXBITS(u,v) >> 1) ^ ((v & 1) ? MAGIC : 0))

static inline uint32_t generate()
{
  size_t idx = state.index;
  if (idx == SIZE) idx = 0;

  if (idx >= DIFF)
  {
    if (idx >= SIZE - 1)
      state.MT[SIZE-1] = state.MT[PERIOD-1] ^ TWIST(state.MT[SIZE-1], state.MT[0]);
    else
      state.MT[idx] = state.MT[idx - DIFF] ^ TWIST(state.MT[idx], state.MT[idx+1]);
  }
  else 
  {
    state.MT[idx] = state.MT[idx + PERIOD] ^ TWIST(state.MT[idx], state.MT[idx+1]);
    /* advance lazy init one slot */
    if (state.index_fast < SIZE)
    {
      state.MT[state.index_fast] = 0x6c078965
        * (state.MT[state.index_fast-1] ^ (state.MT[state.index_fast-1] >> 30))
        + state.index_fast;
      state.index_fast++;
    }
  }

  state.index = idx + 1;
  return state.MT[idx];
}

static inline uint32_t temper(uint32_t y)
{
  y ^= y >> 11;
  y ^= (y << 7) & 0x9d2c5680;
  y ^= (y << 15) & 0xefc60000;
  y ^= y >> 18;
  return y;
}

void mt_seed(uint32_t value)
{
  state.MT[0] = value;
  
  for (uint_fast32_t i = 1; i <= PERIOD; ++i)
    state.MT[i] = 0x6c078965 * (state.MT[i - 1] ^ (state.MT[i - 1] >> 30)) + i;
  
  state.index_fast = PERIOD + 1;
  state.index = SIZE;
}

uint32_t mt_next()
{
  if (state.index > SIZE) {
    /* called without mt_seed(): full init with default seed */
    for (uint_fast32_t i = 1; i < SIZE; ++i)
      state.MT[i] = 0x6c078965 * (state.MT[i - 1] ^ (state.MT[i - 1] >> 30)) + i;
    state.index_fast = SIZE;
    state.index = SIZE;
  }
  
  return temper(generate());
}

void mt_get_state(MTRngState *out)
{
  size_t i;
  for (i = 0; i < SIZE; i++)
    out->MT[i] = state.MT[i];
  out->index = state.index;
  out->index_fast = state.index_fast;
  out->ws = mt_ws;
  out->a = mt_a;
  out->b = mt_b;
  out->initialized = mt_initialized;
}

void mt_set_state(const MTRngState *in)
{
  size_t i;
  for (i = 0; i < SIZE; i++)
    state.MT[i] = in->MT[i];
  state.index = in->index;
  state.index_fast = in->index_fast;
  mt_ws = in->ws;
  mt_a = in->a;
  mt_b = in->b;
  mt_initialized = in->initialized;
}


========================================================================
FILE: vendor/cubiomes-bedrock/mt.h
========================================================================
#ifndef mt_H_
#define mt_H_

#define __STDC_LIMIT_MACROS
#include <stdint.h>
#include <stddef.h>

#ifdef __cplusplus
extern "C"
{
#endif

uint32_t mt_next();
void mt_seed(uint32_t seed_value);

typedef struct MTRngState
{
    uint32_t MT[624];
    size_t index;
    size_t index_fast;
    uint64_t ws;
    int a;
    int b;
    int initialized;
} MTRngState;

void mt_get_state(MTRngState *out);
void mt_set_state(const MTRngState *in);

#ifdef _MSC_VER
extern __declspec(thread) uint64_t mt_ws;
extern __declspec(thread) int mt_a;
extern __declspec(thread) int mt_b;
extern __declspec(thread) int mt_initialized;
#else
extern __thread uint64_t mt_ws;
extern __thread int mt_a;
extern __thread int mt_b;
extern __thread int mt_initialized;
#endif

static inline void setSeed(uint32_t value)
{
    mt_seed(value);
}

static inline int next()
{
    return (int)(mt_next() >> 1);
}

static inline int nextInt(const int n)
{
    return (int)(mt_next() % n);
}

static inline int nextIntRange(const int a, const int b)
{
    return (a < b) ? (a + nextInt(b - a)) : a;
}

static inline int nextBoolean()
{
    return (mt_next() & 0x8000000) != 0;
}

static inline double nextDouble()
{
    return mt_next() * (1.0 / 4294967296.0);
}

static inline float nextFloat()
{
    return (float)nextDouble();
}

static inline void skipNextN(int n)
{
    for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) mt_next();
}

//==============================================================================
// ChunkRandom
//==============================================================================

#define REGION_A 341873128712L
#define REGION_B 132897987541L

static inline int32_t setTerrainSeed(int chunkX, int chunkZ)
{
    uint32_t seed = (chunkX * REGION_A + chunkZ * REGION_B);
    setSeed(seed);
    return seed;
}

static inline uint32_t setPopulationSeed(uint64_t worldSeed, int chunkX, int chunkZ)
{
    if (!mt_initialized || mt_ws != worldSeed)
    {
        setSeed(worldSeed);
        mt_a = next();
        mt_b = next();
        mt_ws = worldSeed;
        mt_initialized = 1;
    }
    uint32_t seed = (chunkX * (mt_a | 1u) + chunkZ * (mt_b | 1u)) ^ worldSeed;
    setSeed(seed);
    return seed;
}

static inline uint32_t setDecorationSeed(uint64_t worldSeed, int chunkX, int chunkZ, int salt)
{
    if (!mt_initialized || mt_ws != worldSeed)
    {
        setSeed(worldSeed);
        mt_a = next();
        mt_b = next();
        mt_ws = worldSeed;
        mt_initialized = 1;
    }
    uint32_t seed = (chunkX * (mt_a | 1u) + chunkZ * (mt_b | 1u)) ^ worldSeed;
    seed = ((seed >> 2) + (seed << 6) + salt - 1640531527u) ^ seed;
    setSeed(seed);
    return seed;
}

static inline uint32_t setCarverSeed(uint64_t worldSeed, int chunkX, int chunkZ)
{
    if (!mt_initialized || mt_ws != worldSeed)
    {
        setSeed(worldSeed);
        mt_a = next();
        mt_b = next();
        mt_ws = worldSeed;
        mt_initialized = 1;
    }
    uint32_t seed = (chunkX * mt_a) ^ (chunkZ * mt_b) ^ worldSeed;
    setSeed(seed);
    return seed;
}

static inline uint32_t setRegionSeed(uint64_t worldSeed, int regX, int regZ, int salt)
{
    uint32_t seed = (regX * REGION_A) + (regZ * REGION_B) + worldSeed + salt;
    setSeed(seed);
    return seed;
}

static inline uint32_t setFortressSeed(uint64_t worldSeed, int chunkX, int chunkZ)
{
    uint32_t seed = ((chunkX >> 4) ^ ((chunkZ >> 4) << 4)) ^ worldSeed;
    setSeed(seed);
    return seed;
}

static inline uint32_t seedSlimeChunk(int chunkX, int chunkZ)
{
    uint32_t seed = (chunkX * 522133279U) ^ chunkZ;
    setSeed(seed);
    return seed;
}

#ifdef __cplusplus
}
#endif

#endif


========================================================================
FILE: tests/test_realm_inclusive_terrain.py
========================================================================
"""Active large-island and inclusive mountain-enclosure preference controls."""
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import pytest
import yaml

from seed_search.realm.config import default_config, validate_config
from seed_search.realm.raster import Raster
from seed_search.realm.terrain import evaluate_v1, evaluate_v2, V1, V2


def grid(bounds=(-768, -768, 768, 768), resolution=8):
    xx, zz = np.meshgrid(np.arange(bounds[0], bounds[2], resolution),
                         np.arange(bounds[1], bounds[3], resolution))
    return bounds, resolution, xx, zz


def island(kind):
    bounds, step, xx, zz = grid()
    land = np.hypot(xx, zz) <= (160 if kind in ('small', 'archipelago') else 400)
    if kind == 'irregular':
        land = (xx / 600) ** 2 + (zz / 180) ** 2 <= 1
    if kind == 'archipelago':
        for x, z in ((-500, 0), (500, 0), (0, -500), (0, 500)):
            land |= np.hypot(xx - x, zz - z) <= 130
    if kind == 'peninsula':
        land |= (xx >= 0) & (np.abs(zz) <= 16)
    return Raster(bounds, step, np.where(land, 85., 50.), water=~land)


def mountains(kind, resolution=8):
    bounds, step, xx, zz = grid((-896, -896, 897, 897), resolution)
    radius = np.hypot(xx, zz)
    angle = np.degrees(np.arctan2(zz, xx)) % 360
    height = np.full(xx.shape, 100.)
    raised = np.zeros(xx.shape, bool)
    if kind in ('separate', 'rough', 'spikes'):
        mountain_radius = 12 if kind == 'spikes' else 135
        for bearing in np.arange(8) * 2 * np.pi / 8:
            raised |= np.hypot(xx - 420 * np.cos(bearing),
                              zz - 420 * np.sin(bearing)) <= mountain_radius
        if kind == 'rough':
            height += np.where(radius < 180, 35 * np.sin(xx / 12) * np.cos(zz / 12), 0)
    elif kind == 'open_pass':
        raised = (radius >= 350) & (radius <= 470) & (angle >= 90)
    elif kind == 'one_side':
        raised = (radius >= 350) & (radius <= 470) & (angle < 120)
    elif kind == 'gentle':
        height += radius / 100
    elif kind == 'flat':
        pass
    else:
        raise ValueError(kind)
    height[raised] = 145
    return Raster(bounds, step, height, water=np.zeros(xx.shape, bool))


def test_default_and_explicit_active_profiles_have_same_terrain_preferences():
    cfg = default_config()
    explicit = yaml.safe_load((Path(__file__).parents[1] / 'config/realm.yaml').read_text())
    assert cfg['terrain']['v1'] == explicit['terrain']['v1']
    assert cfg['terrain']['v2'] == explicit['terrain']['v2']
    for profile in (cfg, explicit):
        validate_config(profile)
        assert profile['terrain']['v1']['area'] == 250000
        assert profile['terrain']['v2']['enclosure_policy'] == 'surrounding_mountains'


@pytest.mark.parametrize('kind', ['large', 'irregular'])
def test_large_non_circular_main_islands_are_accepted(kind):
    result = evaluate_v1(island(kind), (0, 0), thresholds=default_config()['terrain']['v1'])
    assert result['geometry_pass'], result['reasons']
    assert result['metrics']['island_area'] >= 250000
    if kind == 'irregular':
        assert result['metrics']['hull_span'] > 1000
        assert result['metrics']['equivalent_diameter'] < 800


@pytest.mark.parametrize('kind', ['small', 'archipelago', 'peninsula'])
def test_small_island_disconnected_total_and_land_bridge_do_not_pass(kind):
    raster = island(kind)
    result = evaluate_v1(raster, (0, 0), thresholds=default_config()['terrain']['v1'])
    assert not result['geometry_pass']
    if kind == 'archipelago':
        assert raster.cell_areas()[~raster.water_mask()].sum() > 250000
        assert result['metrics']['island_area'] < 100000


@pytest.mark.parametrize('kind', ['separate', 'rough', 'open_pass'])
@pytest.mark.parametrize('resolution', [8, 16, 32])
def test_substantial_surrounding_mountains_and_open_passes_are_accepted(kind, resolution):
    result = evaluate_v2(mountains(kind, resolution), (0, 0),
                         thresholds=default_config()['terrain']['v2'])
    assert result['geometry_pass'], result['reasons']
    assert not result['metrics']['ridge_continuity_policy']['arc_shape_required']
    if kind == 'rough':
        assert result['metrics']['interior_roughness'] > 10


@pytest.mark.parametrize('kind', ['flat', 'gentle', 'one_side', 'spikes'])
def test_inclusive_policy_still_requires_substantial_surrounding_relief(kind):
    result = evaluate_v2(mountains(kind), (0, 0), thresholds=default_config()['terrain']['v2'])
    assert not result['geometry_pass']


def test_legacy_continuous_arc_policy_is_still_selectable():
    raster = mountains('separate')
    assert not evaluate_v2(raster, (0, 0), thresholds=V2)['geometry_pass']
    assert evaluate_v2(raster, (0, 0), thresholds=default_config()['terrain']['v2'])['geometry_pass']
    # Historical low-level controls retain their original size preference.
    assert evaluate_v1(island('small'), (0, 0), thresholds=V1)['geometry_pass']


def test_unknown_coverage_is_not_promoted_by_broader_preferences():
    raster = mountains('separate')
    raster.known[100, 100] = False
    result = evaluate_v2(raster, (0, 0), thresholds=default_config()['terrain']['v2'])
    assert result['qualification'] == 'PENDING'
    assert not result['border_fit']['complete']


def test_enclosure_policy_validation_and_historical_fallback():
    cfg = default_config()
    cfg['terrain']['v2']['enclosure_policy'] = 'anything'
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match='enclosure_policy'):
        validate_config(cfg)
    cfg['terrain']['v2'] = {k: v for k, v in V2.items() if k != 'enclosure_policy'}
    validate_config(cfg)
    result = evaluate_v2(mountains('separate'), (0, 0), thresholds=cfg['terrain']['v2'])
    assert result['metrics']['ridge_continuity_policy']['arc_shape_required']
